# Fix log — pre-production audit (Nov 2026)

Branch `fix/pre-production-audit`, based on the production commit. One entry per audit finding. Statuses: FIXED, PARTIAL, NEEDS-DECISION (no code touched; question and options stated), NOT-CODE (operational, data or process), INVALID, REVERTED-BY-REVIEW. `OTHER-REPO:` marks a change still needed in the other repository. Finding IDs refer to `PRE_PRODUCTION_AUDIT_2026-11-11.md`.

Integration tests (`*IT`) need MongoDB and were not run in the environment where these fixes were written; run the full CI before merging.

## api-00-restore-class

### SCH-01 — FIXED
- Route `POST /api/v1/class-sessions/{id}/restoration` (ADMIN only, same guards as `…/cancellation`: `@PreAuthorize`, tenant and class checks, impersonation → 403 `IMPERSONATION_DENIED`), body `{}`, no `Idempotency-Key` (idempotent by state). 200 → the `ClassSession` schema the cancellation route returns, with `state: ACTIVE`, `cancellation: null`, `counters {booked: 0, waiting: 0}`. Errors: 404 `NOT_FOUND`; 409 `INVALID_STATE` (not CANCELLED, reason DELETED or missing, `startsAt ≤ now`, or a repeat); 409 `CLASS_CONFLICT` (another DRAFT/ACTIVE class of the ring overlaps); 422 `RING_BLOCKED` (ACTIVE ring block); 422 `RING_HAS_BOOKINGS {bookings[]}` (live training booking, never cancelled). A class without a ring skips the ring checks.
- Code: `ClassSessionRules.restorable` (domain; `transition()` unchanged, so CANCELLED stays terminal in the lifecycle), `ClassSessionService.restore` (creation's ring checks moved into `ringFree`, now shared by create/move/restore), `SchedulingAudit.restored` with the new `AuditAction.CLASS_RESTORED` (+ `AuditContracts.AuditActionName`, S14 R-14-09 row, snapshot enum). No booking, waiting-list entry or hold is re-created and nothing is notified; it publishes `ClassSessionUpdated{diff: {state}, bookedCount: 0}` (no N-08b; S09 drops that day's grid cache). Docs: S06 §5/§6/Canvis, `MATRIU_PERMISOS`, `CATALEG_ERRORS` S06 row (+`CLASS_CONFLICT`, reused from S09). `docs/openapi/openapi.json` was edited with a script that reproduces the Jackson snapshot format byte for byte, not regenerated (`bin/openapi-snapshot` needs Docker): regenerate it in CI.
- Tests: `CalendarRulesTest.SCH_01_onlyAFutureCancelledClassThatWasNotDeletedIsRestorable` ran and passed (with a reflection runner, because the Surefire plugin is not in the offline `~/.m2`), as did `AuditContractTest`, `E5RouteParityTest`, `ErrorCatalogContractTest`, the snapshot contract unit tests and the production ArchUnit rules. Not run (MongoDB): `CalendarIT.SCH_01_anAdminRestoresAFutureCancelledClassWithoutItsBookingsNoticesOrAnOccupiedRing` (`@AuditCovers(CLASS_RESTORED)`) and the new route in `e4-routes.json` / `E4ContractIT` (target `cancelled-a`, 57 routes).
- To confirm: class creation does not refuse a second class on the same ring (R-06-05 only flags `RING_DOUBLE_BOOKED`), but restore refuses it with `CLASS_CONFLICT`, following "if the ring is no longer free, refuse". A restored class keeps its `riskExempt` flag, so a manual P2 run on the same day can cancel it again unless the admin exempts it.
- OTHER-REPO: web admin. On a CANCELLED class (D4 calendar / class detail) with `cancellation.reason ≠ DELETED` that has not started, add an ADMIN-only [Restaura la classe] button. Its confirmation says the bookings and the waiting list are not recovered and nobody is notified. The button calls `POST /api/v1/class-sessions/{id}/restoration` with `{}`, replaces the class with the returned `ClassSession` and maps `INVALID_STATE` / `CLASS_CONFLICT` / `RING_BLOCKED` / `RING_HAS_BOOKINGS` to their messages. Also add the `CLASS_RESTORED` label (ca/es/en) to the audit log's action names and regenerate the API types from the snapshot.
- Review note: reviewed by reading the diff and by exercising `ClassSessionService.restore` with mocked ports (every refusal, the restored snapshot, the event payload); the two IT cases and the hand-edited snapshot still need CI. Correction: the `docs/openapi/CHANGELOG.md` entry that every endpoint change requires was missing and is added. For the owner: (1) the `CLASS_CONFLICT` refusal above is stricter than class creation; (2) restore does not re-check that the class's ring, levels and instructors are still active: they can be deactivated while the class is cancelled (R-05-07 counts only live classes), and the restored class is then flagged `RING_INACTIVE` / `LEVEL_INACTIVE` / `INSTRUCTOR_INACTIVE` in the calendar instead of being refused; (3) the `risk` marks from before the cancellation are kept, so P2 does not warn the admins a second time about a restored class.

## api-01-high-access

### AUTHZ-01 — FIXED
- `CensusLists.dataset`: the members list's searchable paths drop `idDocument.number` when the reader is not ADMIN (`ListAccess.admin()`), next to the existing removal of the `idDocument` column. `q` on `GET /api/v1/members` and `/members/filter-values` (same definition) can no longer match the ID number for an INSTRUCTOR; ADMIN search and the dogs list are unchanged. Spec: S03 R-03-22 amended (+ Canvis line). No OpenAPI change (searchable paths are not published).
- Tests: new unit test `CensusListsSearchTest` (3 cases; ran and passed with a reflection runner, Surefire is not in the offline `~/.m2`; the instructor case fails without the fix). New IT case `UniversalListsIT.AUTHZ_01_instructorSearchMatchingOnlyAnIdNumberReturnsNoRows` (list, `id` filter + `q`, filter-values, name/e-mail search still working): not run (MongoDB).
- No web change needed: the instructor UI searches students through `GET /dogs?q`, not `/members?q`.
- Review note: confirmed by reading the list engine (`MongoListRepository.search` / `selection` / `facets`, `ListQuery`) and re-running `CensusListsSearchTest` (3 pass; the instructor case fails against a build without the fix). The IT case was checked by hand against its fixture and still needs CI. After the change no filter, sort or search path of `/members` reaches the ID number for an INSTRUCTOR, and the behaviour matches the published description of `q` ("within the caller's permitted projection"). For the owner: the amended R-03-22 says the instructor does not see the ID document and cites R-03-31, but neither that rule's list of withheld fields nor `MATRIU_PERMISOS` names it; the withholding comes from the published `MemberInstructorView` (no `idDocument`) and the code. Confirm that instructors are not meant to see ID numbers. The amendment exists only in this repository's copy of S03; the web repository keeps its own copy.

### AUTH-02 — FIXED
- Verified, no code touched: every system e-mail (N-25 login link, password recovery, staff "resend access") is gated on `Account.emailStatus == null` (`SystemNotificationService.java:120`); the Brevo webhook sets BOUNCED/COMPLAINED on `hard_bounce`, `invalid_email`, `blocked`, `spam` (`BrevoWebhookService.java:26,122-127`); `AccountRepository.markEmailStatus` (:63-67) is the only writer and nothing clears it; `Account.email` has no change path (S01 R-01-01's verified change from `/account` and `AccountEmailChanged` are not built).
- To decide: who may change a member's login e-mail and how. Options: (a) ADMIN action on D10 "change login e-mail" with a magic link to the new address, notice to the old one (R-01-01's N-26 variant), audited, emits `AccountEmailChanged` and clears `emailStatus` (needs an audit-action entry in the closed catalogs); (b) only a staff "clear e-mail suppression" action for an address known to be good again; (c) build R-01-01's self-service change (does not help a member who cannot sign in). Also: keep Brevo `blocked` permanent (today it follows R-11-08's `dropped`) or treat it as temporary, and whether D10 shows the account's suppressed state.
- Meanwhile (NOT-CODE): put the manual Mongo fix (`accounts.email`, unset `emailStatus`; unique index `account_email`) in the runbook and review `ACCOUNT_EMAIL_STATUS_CHANGED` audit entries daily during launch week.

Pass 2 (owner decision of 13 Nov 2026): an ADMIN changes a member's login e-mail from D10; the link goes to the NEW address only, the change takes effect when it is used, is audited with a new action and clears `emailStatus`; no notice to the old address; D10 shows the bounce/complaint mark; Brevo `blocked` is temporary.
- Route `POST /api/v1/members/{id}/login-email` (`MembersController.changeLoginEmail`): ADMIN, no impersonation, closed to a support token (`@SupportRestricted` + `SupportAccessService.RESTRICTED`), body `{email}` (`LoginEmailChangeRequest`, `@Email`, ≤ 254), no `Idempotency-Key` (as `access-resend`), `202 {sentTo}` (`AccessResendResponse`, the new address). Checks in `MemberService.loginEmail` → `CensusIdentityService.checkLoginEmailChange`: member `ACTIVE` with an account and an active membership (`422 MEMBER_NOT_ACTIVE`), `404`/`409 MEMBER_ERASED | INVALID_STATE` from `mutableMember`, `403 FORBIDDEN` for an account that also has another club's membership or a platform role, `400 VALIDATION_ERROR fieldErrors[{email, UNCHANGED}]` for the current address, `429` from the magic-link buckets of `/auth/magic-link` (`limitResend`: the new address and the administrator's IP). Then, outside the request's transaction, `MagicLinkService.sendEmailChange` stores a `MagicLinkToken` of the new purpose `EMAIL_CHANGE` (new fields `email`, `requestedByAccountId`; lifetime `auth.welcomeLinkDays`; `supersede` ends the account's earlier unused change links; `MagicLinkRequested{purpose: EMAIL_CHANGE}` as for any link) and `SystemNotificationService.sendEmailChange` mails N-25 variant `EMAIL_CHANGE` (`/activacio?t=…&purpose=email_change`, new copy `notif.N-25.emailChange.*` in ca/es/en, expiry from the link) to the new address only; the account's suppression mark belongs to the old address and does not stop it. An address of another account gets no token and no mail, with the same 202.
- Redemption: `TokenService.magicLink` (the existing magic-link grant, so the web's `/activacio` needs no new call) asks `AccountEmailChangeService.applicable` before consuming (another account took the address since → `MAGIC_LINK_INVALID`, nothing changes), then `apply`: `AccountRepository.changeEmail` (new address, `emailVerifiedAt = now`, `emailStatus` unset; the `account_email` unique index stays the last guard) and one `ACCOUNT_EMAIL_CHANGED` entry through the new `AuditWriter.writeAs` (the requesting ADMIN as actor, role ADMIN, origin BACKOFFICE, `entityType = Account`, member of the club, `changes` email and emailStatus; the link holder's IP and browser are not put on the ADMIN's entry, only the request's trace id). The session it opens carries the new address.
- D10: the ADMIN `Member` view (`GET`/`PATCH /members/{id}`, also D2 and reactivation) has `login {email, emailStatus: BOUNCED | COMPLAINED | null}` (`CensusIdentityService.login`), null without an account or after erasure.
- Brevo: `BrevoWebhookService.TEMPORARY` = `soft_bounce`, `deferred`, `blocked`; `blocked` records the temporary incident (sanitized reason) and no longer suppresses the address, marks the contact e-mail bounced or sets `Account.emailStatus`.
- Catalogues: `AuditAction` and `AuditContracts.AuditActionName` gain `ACCOUNT_EMAIL_CHANGED`; route in `e2-routes.json` and `handler-roles.txt` (regenerated); OpenAPI snapshot regenerated with `bin/openapi-snapshot` (+ `docs/openapi/CHANGELOG.md`); `docs/DEPLOY.md` Brevo paragraph.
- Tests: new `LoginEmailChangeIT` (5: the link to the new address only, nothing changed before it is used, the redemption changes and verifies the address, clears `BOUNCED`, keeps the contact e-mails and writes one ADMIN `ACCOUNT_EMAIL_CHANGED` (`@AuditCovers`), a used link refused; a newer request supersedes; an address taken before use changes nothing; another account's address gets no mail; refusals UNCHANGED / invalid / PENDING / shared account / platform role / INSTRUCTOR / unknown member; another club's ADMIN gets 404) and `LoginEmailChangeTest` (10 unit cases of the checks, the D10 view, the send and `AccountEmailChangeService`); `SystemEmailBrandingTest` +1 (the variant's subject in ca/es/en and the 168 h expiry); `BrevoWebhookIT` +1 `AUTH_02_blockedIsATemporaryFailureThatNeverSuppressesTheAddressOrTheAccount`. Changed test: `BrevoWebhookIT.T_11_08_permanentFailuresSuppressAndKeepAccountSemantics` listed `blocked` among the permanent failures; it now covers `spam`, `hard_bounce`, `invalid_email` and the new test asserts the decided temporary behaviour. Run and passing: `LoginEmailChangeTest`, `SystemEmailBrandingTest`, `CensusIdentityServiceSurvivorsTest`, `HandlerRolesSnapshotTest`, `AuditContractTest`, `MessageParityTest`; ITs `LoginEmailChangeIT`, `BrevoWebhookIT`, `SecurityInventoryIT`, `E2ContractIT`, `OpenApiSnapshotTest`, `CensusIT`, `IdentityCoreIT`, `SupportAccessIT`, `AuditedAspectIT`, `MigrationWelcomeIT`, `DisabledEmailIdentityIT`. Whole unit suite after both findings: 501 classes, 5076 tests, 0 failures (baseline 499 / 5062).
- Assumption: one new audit action, written when the change takes effect (R-14-09: in the transaction of the change), as the requesting ADMIN's entry (the decision makes it an ADMIN action); an unused request changes nothing and leaves no audit entry, as an unused sign-in link.
- Assumption: a club changes only an account that is its own alone and has no platform role (`403 FORBIDDEN`), as R-01-14 renames only such an account: otherwise one club's admin would move another club's member, or a platform administrator, to an address of their choice.
- Assumption: the route is closed to a support token (S17 R-17-10's list), like a member's impersonation: the change hands the account to whoever holds the new address.
- Assumption: the new address must differ from the current one (`UNCHANGED`); the owner chose a change of address, not a staff "clear the mark" action, so re-entering the same address does not clear `emailStatus`.
- Assumption: an address that already belongs to another account gets no link and the ADMIN gets the same 202 (CATALEG_ERRORS §3 rule 3: no message reveals another person's e-mail); `EMAIL_EXISTS` is not used.
- Assumption: the link lasts `auth.welcomeLinkDays` (7) like the other links an ADMIN sends from D10 (`ACCESS_RESEND`), and the request uses the limits of `/auth/magic-link` that `access-resend` had when this was written (10/h per new address, 60/h per IP); WEB-01's per-club bucket is the welcome's, as its decision says.
- Assumption: no `AccountEmailChanged` event is emitted (the decision does not ask for one and nothing consumes it); the change, not the request, is what the audit records.
- Assumption: the provider's `blocked` is Brevo's event (production provider). SendGrid's `bounce` of type `blocked` on a SYSTEM row still marks the account, unchanged.

- SPEC-CHANGE: docs/specs/S01-agilityhub-id-i-acces.md R-01-01: the ADMIN's change from D10 besides `/account`, without a notice to the old address; §8 N-25 gains `EMAIL_CHANGE`; Canvis.
- SPEC-CHANGE: docs/specs/S11-comunicacions.md R-11-08: Brevo `blocked` is temporary (no suppression of the address, the contact or the account); the 01-11 operating note; Canvis.
- SPEC-CHANGE: docs/specs/S14-tauler-auditoria-exportacions-rgpd.md R-14-09: audit action `ACCOUNT_EMAIL_CHANGED` added; Canvis.

- OTHER-REPO: web clubs-admin D10 (`CensusRecordPage`): show `member.login.email` in «Dades i pagament» with a badge when `login.emailStatus` is `BOUNCED` («rebot»: the address bounces or does not exist) or `COMPLAINED` («queixa»: marked as spam), explaining that sign-in links, recovery and the welcome do not reach it; an ADMIN action «Canvia l'adreça d'accés» (hidden without `login`) opening a dialog with the current address and a new-address field, confirmation, then `POST /api/v1/members/{id}/login-email` `{email}` → `202 {sentTo}` toast «Enllaç de confirmació enviat a {sentTo}; l'adreça canviarà quan l'obri»; map `VALIDATION_ERROR` (`fieldErrors` email `UNCHANGED` → «és l'adreça actual»; invalid address), `MEMBER_NOT_ACTIVE` 422, `FORBIDDEN` 403 («aquest compte no es pot canviar des del club»), `SUPPORT_RESTRICTED` 403, `MEMBER_ERASED` 409, `RATE_LIMITED` 429 (`details.retryAfter`); texts in ca/es/en. `/activacio?t=…&purpose=email_change` already works through the magic-link grant; optionally title it «La teva adreça d'accés ha canviat». Audit log (D9): the `ACCOUNT_EMAIL_CHANGED` label in ca/es/en. Regenerate the API types from the snapshot.
- NOT-CODE: accounts already marked `BOUNCED` by a Brevo `blocked` before this change keep the mark (no data migration was decided). The webhook receipts and their `ACCOUNT_EMAIL_STATUS_CHANGED` entries tell which; with a correct address the only in-app way out is a change of address, otherwise the operator unsets `accounts.emailStatus` by hand after checking.
- Review note: reviewed f0240bb and e0fa6dc against the decision (the route and its checks, the redemption in `TokenService.magicLink`, the D10 `login` view, the Brevo change, the catalogues, the spec copies and the snapshot). It implements the decision. Build green. Run again and passing: unit `LoginEmailChangeTest`, `SystemEmailBrandingTest`, `AuditContractTest`, `HandlerRolesSnapshotTest`, `CensusIdentityServiceSurvivorsTest`, `MessageParityTest`, `ErrorCatalogContractTest`, `RateLimitsTest`; ITs `LoginEmailChangeIT`, `BrevoWebhookIT`, `SecurityInventoryIT`, `E2ContractIT`, `OpenApiSnapshotTest` (the committed snapshot matches), `CensusIT`, `IdentityCoreIT`, `SupportAccessIT`, `AuditedAspectIT`. No address, token or link reaches the test logs. For the owner: (1) the route accepts any account that belongs only to this club, staff included. An ADMIN can move another ADMIN's or an INSTRUCTOR's login to an address the ADMIN controls, open the link and be signed in as that person with all of their roles. Impersonation gives only the MEMBER role. The audit entry names the requesting ADMIN and is written when the link is used. Excluding staff accounts, or requiring a second admin for them, would be a further decision. (2) "Blocked is temporary" is implemented for Brevo only. `deploy/compose.prod.yaml` defaults `EMAIL_PROVIDER` to `sendgrid`. Under SendGrid, a `bounce` of type `blocked` on a system e-mail (sign-in link, recovery, welcome) still marks the account `BOUNCED` (`SendGridWebhookService.system`). Confirm that production sets `EMAIL_PROVIDER=brevo`; if it does not, the SendGrid path needs the same change. (3) The change applies to the whole AgilityHub ID account, so it also changes the sign-in address for any other product that uses that account. Accounts linked to Learn (`externalIds`, `createdSource = IMPORT_LEARN`) are not excluded. (4) For an address that already belongs to another account, nothing is sent, but the ADMIN gets the same 202 and the web's toast still says the link was sent.

### PRIV-01 — FIXED
- Verified, no code touched: the mandatory first-login acceptance links to `agilityhub.legal.privacyPolicyUrl` (default `https://id.agilitydoghub.com/privacy`, `application.yml:68-69`, `deploy/compose.prod.yaml:54-55`), which apps/id does not serve (SPA "not found" card, HTTP 200); `docs/legal/*` are unreviewed drafts with `[…]` placeholders and the wrong sub-processors (no Brevo/DigitalOcean/Stripe).
- To decide (legal): the reviewed platform policy text and its processors/retention; where it lives, either (a) a `/privacy` route in apps/id (OTHER-REPO work once the text exists) or (b) a static page with `PLATFORM_PRIVACY_POLICY_URL` pointing at it; and the version string (changing `PLATFORM_PRIVACY_POLICY_VERSION` later re-prompts every account). The club must confirm its own policy page (`club.legal.privacyPolicyUrl`, version "1") covers this platform. Then click the link on the production onboarding screen.


Pass 2 (owner decision of 13 Nov 2026): `apps/id` serves `/privacy` (ca/es/en) from a versioned content file carrying the definitive text (the club is the controller of all the data; AgilityHub, DigitalOcean, Brevo and Stripe are processors; the bank receives the SEPA remittances); its effective date is a placeholder that fails the production build; the server's `PLATFORM_PRIVACY_POLICY_URL` defaults to that route and its version string matches the file; `docs/legal` carries the same text.
- This repository's side: `application.yml` `agilityhub.legal.privacyPolicyVersion` defaults to `2` (was `v1`), the version of the web's content file (`apps/id/src/privacy/privacy-policy.ts` `PRIVACY_POLICY_VERSION`, web commits 4fc9b7e and b16c50b); `deploy/compose.prod.yaml`, `deploy/.env.prod.example` and `.env.example` likewise. `PLATFORM_PRIVACY_POLICY_URL` already defaulted to `https://id.agilitydoghub.com/privacy`, the `/privacy` route on Caddy's `ID_HOST` (`/srv/id`), the issuer's host: verified, unchanged. `docs/legal/POLITICA_PRIVACITAT_AGILITYHUB_ID.md` and `docs/legal/README.md` are now byte-identical to the web's (the definitive ca/es/en text, «Versió: 2», effective date pending). `docs/DEPLOY.md` row of the two variables rewritten. The effective-date constant and the failing production build are the web's (`apps/id`, `release-guard.ts`); the API stores only the version on each consent and needs no date.
- The text was not written by a lawyer and needs the club's legal review before its effective date is set (as its header says).
- Tests: new `LegalPolicyDefaultsTest` (2: every server default carries the document's version `2` and the `/privacy` URL on the ID host, and the Caddyfile serves `apps/id` there; the copy is the definitive text with the owner's controller data and processors and none of the draft markers, Twilio, SendGrid, AWS, Sentry or Learn). Changed: a comment of `AccountEmailServiceSurvivorsTest` naming the old default. Run and passing: unit `LegalPolicyDefaultsTest`, `OnboardingServiceSurvivorsTest`, `AccountEmailServiceSurvivorsTest`; ITs `OnboardingIT` (27), `IdentityContractIT` (11). No route or schema changed: no snapshot regeneration.
- Assumption: the API-side checks of the text's statements were limited to what this repository decides (retention values, the 30-day backup lifecycle, sealed IBANs, argon2id passwords, HIBP's k-anonymity call, the card's brand and last four digits, the signup consents' IP hash, security events and audit entries with IP and browser, the support access): they hold. The copy is not edited here so that both repositories keep one text.
- SPEC-CHANGE: docs/specs/S01-agilityhub-id-i-acces.md §2 `apps/id` row (+ `/privacy`), §14 onboarding paragraph (the `PLATFORM` policy is `apps/id` `/privacy` with `PLATFORM_PRIVACY_POLICY_VERSION`), Canvis — the same lines as the web's copy.
- SPEC-CHANGE: docs/specs/S14-tauler-auditoria-exportacions-rgpd.md R-14-18: the processors are the platform policy's (club = controller; AgilityHub, DigitalOcean (EU), Brevo, Stripe; the bank); Canvis — as the web's copy.
- NOT-CODE: deploy. (1) A private production `.env` that still sets `PLATFORM_PRIVACY_POLICY_VERSION=v1` overrides the new default: set it to `2` (or remove it). (2) Version `2` asks every account that accepted `v1` to accept again (S01 §14, postponable `legal.maxPostpones` times). (3) The text names Brevo for e-mail and SMS, DigitalOcean for servers, database, files and backups, and no Sentry: `deploy/compose.prod.yaml` defaults to SendGrid and Twilio and `deploy/.env.prod.example` points the export, attachment and backup buckets at an AWS region (`eu-west-3`, no endpoint). Production must run with `EMAIL_PROVIDER=brevo`, `SMS_PROVIDER=brevo`, DigitalOcean Spaces endpoints in the EU (or the droplet volume) and an empty `SENTRY_DSN`, or the text and its version change. (4) `apps/id` cannot be built for production until the club fixes the effective date after its legal review.
### PRIV-02 — FIXED
- Verified, no code touched: `audit.retentionYears`, `rgpd.retentionYearsAfterLeave`, `rgpd.erasureMinDaysAfterLeave`, `rgpd.rejectedSignupRetentionDays` are club-editable with `min: 0` (`catalog.yaml:1503-1555`); a club ADMIN can then run `retention-sweep` on demand (`JobsController.java:72-80`) and purge the audit trail, erase every LEFT member and delete remittance XML files of any status (`PaymentsErasureRepository.remittanceFilesBefore`, no status filter, as R-14-16 (c) is written).
- To decide: (1) legal floors in the catalog and CATALEG_PARAMETRES (e.g. 6 years for audit and after-leave retention per the Codi de Comerç art. 30 cited there, or a lower minimum such as 1) or `editableBy: PLATFORM` for the four keys; (2) whether R-14-16 (c) should keep the file of a remittance still `GENERATED` (not yet submitted) — a spec change; (3) whether a manual retention sweep needs a server-side dry run with counts and a confirmation, or a cap per run, or is reserved to the platform. The parameter catalogue is compared with the code by tests: change both sides together.
- NOT-CODE: remove the shared QA administrator account from production before launch.

Pass 2 (owner decision of 13 Nov 2026): floors in the parameter catalogue (audit ≥ 6 years, after leaving ≥ 1 year, erasure margin ≥ 30 days; still club-editable above them); a manual retention sweep needs a server-side dry run and the explicit confirmation of its counts before anything is deleted; the sweep never deletes the file of a remittance still `GENERATED`.
- Floors: `catalog.yaml` `constraints.min` 6 (`audit.retentionYears`), 1 (`rgpd.retentionYearsAfterLeave`), 30 (`rgpd.erasureMinDaysAfterLeave`); `editableBy` stays `CLUB`. `PUT /parameters/{key}` under the floor → `400 PARAMETER_INVALID` (existing `ParameterValidator`); an override already stored under it is ignored by `ClubConfigService` (`ParameterInvalidOverride`), so the product default applies. `GET /parameters` publishes the new `constraints.min`.
- Confirmed dry run: `POST /jobs/{name}/trigger` and the console's `POST /platform/clubs/{clubId}/jobs/{name}/trigger` take an optional `confirmation {dryRunId, counters}` (`JobViews.JobTriggerRequest`, new schema `JobRunConfirmation`). For a process whose `Job.manualRunNeedsConfirmedDryRun()` is true (only `RetentionSweepJob`), `JobTriggerService.confirmedPlan` requires a `SUCCEEDED` dry run of the same process in the club started at most 30 minutes before (`CONFIRMATION_WINDOW`) and counts equal to that dry run's; it compares `JobViews.plannedCounts` (the `WOULD_*` counters other than zero, without the platform pass ones). `JobRunner.manual(…, confirmedPlan)` then plans again in a dry context under the process lock, before the RUNNING row or anything else is written, and refuses a different plan. Every refusal is `409 SIMULATION_STALE {reason: DRY_RUN_REQUIRED | COUNTS_MISMATCH | PLAN_CHANGED}`, with no run and no audit entry. The sweep's dry run also counts the audit entries its single `PURGE_AUDIT` item would remove (`WOULD_PURGE_AUDIT_ENTRIES`). The calendar's monthly run is unchanged.
- Remittance files: `PaymentsErasureRepository.remittanceFilesBefore`, `remittanceFile` and `remittanceFilePurged` exclude `status = GENERATED`, so a forged or stale item cannot delete it either.

Changed tests: `OpenApiSnapshotTest.T_15_09_jobRoutesPublishNo422ThatNoCodeMapsTo` and `INC_47_INC_43_keyedRoutesShareTheStaleVersionConflictAndDownloadsDeclareTheirRanges` asserted the trigger's old 409 list; they now expect `JOB_ALREADY_RUNNING, SIMULATION_STALE, IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_REUSED, STALE_VERSION`. ## api-02-high-money

### SEPA-02 — PARTIAL
- Verified, no code touched. At cut-over every SEPA member gets the reference `{clubSlug}-{memberNumber}-1`, signed on the cut-over date (`PlayoffPlanner.java:298-319`; the optional mandates file read at 309-318 was not supplied). An admin change of account mints the next sequence, signed `clock.instant()` (`MemberService.java:186-197`). Sign-up stamps the submission time (`SignupService.java:391`). No acceptance record, mandate text version or signed document is stored, and the pain.008 carries only `MndtId` + `DtOfSgntr` (`Pain008Document.java:175-177`). All of this is prescribed: S18 R-18-08 and B6, E43 with S04 R-04-10, and E8-T03 on S12 §7.
- To decide, with the bank (A36): (a) continue PlayOff's existing mandates by loading their real references and signature dates through `mandats.csv` with an approved reapply (its headings are rehearsal placeholders, see MIG-06); or (b) keep the new references with the bank's written acceptance and collect new mandates with stored evidence: a logged acceptance (text version, timestamp, last 4 IBAN digits, channel) at sign-up and on `/me`, or an uploaded signed PDF. (b) needs a new model, API and screens.
- Also to decide: what an admin account change means. Options: keep today's rule (valid only if the admin holds a signed paper mandate); leave the new mandate unsigned (`mandateSignedAt = null` → `MANDATE_PENDING`, which the run skips until the member signs; this needs a signing flow); or let the admin enter the paper mandate's signature date in the PATCH.


Pass 2 (owner decision of 13 Nov 2026): continue PlayOff's existing mandates — the import loads the real references and signature dates from the mandates file, validates them, lists every SEPA member without a row for the operator, and an approved reapply updates the mandates of members already imported; no new-mandate capture model. PARTIAL: everything is done except replacing the rehearsal headings of `mandats.csv`, which needs PlayOff's real mandates export (MIG-06); it is not in this repository and must never be.
- `PlayoffPlanner`: `mandates()` validates every `mandats.csv` row after the destinations are resolved and before anything is planned; `mandatesOfOtherMembers()` checks the references against the members this load does not write. A row is `members ERROR INPUT_SCHEMA_MISMATCH` with field `mandateRef` (outside the EPC rules: longer than 35, outside the SEPA set, a leading or trailing `/` or `//`; or a reference another member already has, in the file or stored on a member the load does not write, case-insensitive), `mandateSignedAt` (missing, unreadable, or after the club's today) or `memberId` (a second, different mandate for the same member). The same mandate twice is not an error; an empty reference is no mandate. The report never prints the reference. A row reaches the member through any record of the person (one record per dog, a `persones.csv` join), as `pagat_fins.csv` does. In `memberAndDog` a SEPA member takes the file's reference and date or none: the generated `{clubSlug}-{memberNumber}-1` signed on the cut-over, the kept-on-reapply branch and the number dependency are gone; without a row → `mandateRef`/`mandateSignedAt` null and `members WARNING MANDATE_PENDING` (S12's simulation lists it and the run skips it, E156). The replaced mandate's `{slug}-{number}-n` sequence is carried over in `lastMandateSequence` (`CensusMigrationService.keepMandateSequence`, E92) so a later new mandate never reuses a reference an earlier load stored. A reapply (step 5, approved in production) writes the same values, so it replaces what an earlier load stored.
- `CensusRules.validMandateRef` (new) holds the reference rule; `CensusMigrationService` exposes it and `keepMandateSequence`, and loses `mandateRef(slug, number)` and `usableMandateRef`, now unused.
- Tests: unit `CensusRulesTest.SEPA_02_aMandateReferenceOfThePreviousSystemFollowsTheEpcRules`; ITs `PlayoffMigrationIT.SEPA_02_aMandatesRowThatBreaksTheRulesBlocksTheLoadOnItsRowAndField` (each rule on its row and field, nothing written, the reference never in the report, then the clean file loads), `PlayoffMigrationIT.R_18_08_SEPA_02_theMandatesFileGivesEachMemberItsPreviousMandateAndALaterLoadReplacesIt` (a `NUMBER_CONFLICT` record and a second record of a person get the file's mandate, an empty reference gives `MANDATE_PENDING`, S12's simulation bills the one and skips the other, a reload replaces and removes), `MigrationStagedImportIT.SEPA_02_anApprovedReapplyOfStep5ReplacesTheStoredMandatesWithThePreviousSystemsOnes` (production load, invented mandates stored, approved reapply of step 5 only: the file's mandate replaces it with `lastMandateSequence` 1, a member without a row loses it and is listed, step 3's fields untouched). Changed to assert the decided behaviour: `T_18_04_explicitPayerGroupsEncryptedBankAndNewCutoverMandates` → `T_18_04_explicitPayerGroupsEncryptedBankAndNoInventedMandate` (asserted `{slug}-{number}-1` signed on the cut-over); `R_18_08_E43_aNumberConflictSepaRecordGetsNoMandateUntilItHasANumber` → `R_18_08_E43_SEPA_02_withoutTheMandatesFileNoSepaMemberGetsAnInventedMandate` (asserted four `MANDATE_PENDING` lines, now one per SEPA member); `R_18_08_E156_aNumberConflictFixedInPlayoffGetsASignedMandateOnTheNextLoad` replaced by the R_18_08_SEPA_02 test above (the mandate no longer depends on the number); `T_18_07_theNextMonthsSimulationReconcilesWithTheForecastWithinOnePercent` now writes a fictional mandates file for every SEPA member (without one no SEPA member would be billed). Run and passing: unit `CensusRulesTest` (8), `PlayoffAdapterTest` (11), `MigrationCensusStepsTest` (11), `MigrationApplyStepsTest` (4), `PlayoffExecutionCommandTest` (7), `ArchitectureRulesTest` (23); ITs `PlayoffMigrationIT` (53; the validation test re-run alone after correcting its 36-character reference), `MigrationStagedImportIT` (12).
- Assumption: «date not in the future» is checked against the club's today when the import runs (not the cut-over): a reapply weeks after the cut-over may load a mandate the club signed on paper after it.
- Assumption: a SEPA member without a row gets no mandate at all (`MANDATE_PENDING`, skipped by the run) rather than a generated one: the decision continues the PlayOff mandates and the operator must resolve each listed member; keeping an invented reference would leave the audit's exposure in place. The same rule applies on a reapply, so a reapply without a member's row removes the mandate stored for it.
- Assumption: invalid rows are errors that block the load (as an unreadable date already was), not warnings; a short or broken row is still a warning (R-18-11b (h)).
- Assumption: uniqueness is the SEPA one (a reference identifies one mandate of the creditor): two members in the file, or a file reference equal to one held by a member this load does not write (a sign-up after the cut-over), ignoring case.
- For the owner: (1) the real headings of PlayOff's mandates export are still unknown; until they are, the operator writes them in the `--mapping` (or the file is reshaped to `Member ID;Mandate reference;Signed on`). (2) A step-5 reapply rewrites the whole payment block from the export: IBAN, holder, plan, price and `nextInvoiceDate` too (R-18-14 and `MIGRATION_STEPS.md`, unchanged). After a billing run has moved a member's `nextInvoiceDate`, the reapply moves it back to the cut-over's and the next run bills those months again; run it before the first billing run, or correct the dates with `PUT /members/{id}/next-invoice-date`. The runbook says so (`docs/DEPLOY.md`). (3) If a remittance has already gone out under the generated references, the next debit carries another `MndtId` for the same debtor; the pain.008 has no amendment block (`AmdmntInd`/`OrgnlMndtId`), which was not part of this decision. (4) Admin and sign-up mandate creation is unchanged (no capture model, as decided).
- SPEC-CHANGE: docs/specs/S18-migracio-playoff.md R-18-08: the mandates come from the mandates file (reference and date, validated: EPC identifier rules, one reference per member, date not after today), no generated mandate, `MANDATE_PENDING` for every SEPA member without a row, an approved reapply of step 5 replaces the stored mandates and keeps the sequence; T-18-04 and T-18-14 descriptions; §13 doubt 2 annotated; Canvis.

- OTHER-REPO: none (the import is a CLI command; no API route or schema changed).
- Review note (p2-api-03-sepa-high, 13 Nov 2026): reviewed e3dde4c; the code does what the decision asks (file mandates validated, nothing invented, every SEPA member without a row listed as `MANDATE_PENDING`, an approved step-5 reapply replaces stored mandates) and `CensusRulesTest`, `PlayoffMigrationIT`, `MigrationStagedImportIT` pass. For the owner: (1) the load now depends entirely on PlayOff's real mandates export. Without `mandats.csv`, or with wrong headings in the `--mapping`, every SEPA member is loaded without a mandate and the billing run skips them all, so no SEPA debit goes out until the file is loaded and step 5 is reapplied. Read the dry run's `MANDATE_PENDING` count before applying. (2) A reapply also removes the stored mandate of every member who has no row in the file. (3) The real headings (MIG-06) are still open, which is why the entry is PARTIAL.

### SEPA-04 — FIXED
- Verified, no code touched. P5i settles every `SUBMITTED` remittance on its collection date without any bank input (`BillingExpirations.java:37-77`). Nothing leads back from `SUBMITTED` (`BillingDocuments.java:255-265`, `BillingRunService.java:262`). Returns are recorded one invoice at a time (`InvoiceActions.java:84-99`), and a FAILED periodic invoice never joins a later run (`BillingDocuments.java:155-158`). This is what S12 R-12-15, R-12-17 and BR-08 prescribe.
- To decide (a spec change, plus new audit actions and error codes in the closed catalogs): (a) an ADMIN "undo sent" `SUBMITTED → GENERATED`, allowed before the collection date while no invoice of the remittance has been touched; (b) a "bank rejected the file" action that fails the whole remittance; (c) a bulk "mark returned" with one N-10 summary instead of one per invoice; (d) settle only after an explicit "bank confirmed" step, or after D+N; (e) let a FAILED periodic invoice re-enter a later remittance.
- Meanwhile (NOT-CODE): change the runbook line "Mark the remittance as sent the day you upload it" (`docs/DEPLOY.md:1115`) to "mark it once the bank has accepted the file", and reconcile returns against the bank statement by D+5.


Pass 2 (owner decision of 13 Nov 2026): (a) an ADMIN «undo sent» `SUBMITTED → GENERATED`, before the collection date and while no receipt of the remittance was touched, audited; (b) a bulk «mark returned» of several receipts of one remittance with ONE summary notice, and a FAILED periodic receipt can re-enter a later remittance; (c) receipts become PAID 5 TARGET business days after the collection date (catalogue parameter, default 5), not on the day. Not chosen and not built: a «bank rejected the file» action. Three commits: (a) 2088d8f, (c) 17cbc52, (b) the last one, with this log.
- (a) `POST /api/v1/remittances/{id}/submission-undo` (`RemittancesController.undoRemittanceSubmission`; ADMIN without impersonation and module `BILLING` like its neighbours; `Idempotency-Key` required through `BillingTransactions.keyed`; body `SubmissionUndoRequest {reason}`, 1–500 characters; `200 Remittance`). `RemittanceService.undoSubmission`: not `SUBMITTED` → `409 INVALID_STATE {status}`; club-local today not before `requestedCollectionDate` → `409 INVALID_STATE {reason: COLLECTION_DATE_PASSED}`; any receipt of the remittance's collections that is not `COLLECTING` in this remittance with its attempt there still open (`CREATED`/`SUBMITTED`) and last → `409 INVALID_STATE {reason: INVOICE_CHANGED, invoiceIds}`; then `RemittanceRepository.undoSubmission` (status-guarded, `submittedAt` and `submittedByAccountId` unset; a lost race → `409 STALE_VERSION`) and one `REMITTANCE_SUBMISSION_UNDONE` entry (`AuditAction` and the contract's `AuditActionName`) with the reason and `details {runId, period, messageId, submittedOn}`. Nothing else moves: the receipts stay `COLLECTING`, the run is rollbackable again (R-12-14) and `submissionDue` is shown again.
- (c) `TargetCalendar` (payments domain: Monday–Friday minus 1 January, Good Friday, Easter Monday, 1 May, 25 and 26 December; Easter by the Gregorian computus). `BillingExpirations` (P5i) reads the new parameter `billing.sepa.settlementBusinessDays` (`catalog.yaml`: int, default 5, 0–40, block billing, module `BILLING`, CLUB-editable) through `JobContext.parameter`, so the run records it, and settles a `SUBMITTED` remittance from `requestedCollectionDate` + that many TARGET business days (0 = the collection date, the former behaviour). `paidAt` stays the start of the collection date.
- (b) `POST /api/v1/remittances/{id}/returns` (`RemittancesController.markRemittanceReturns`; same roles, module, key and tenant checks as its neighbours, the invoices checked one by one by `BillingContractAccess.invoices`; body `RemittanceReturnsRequest {invoiceIds[], reason, at, includeInNextRun?}`; `200 RemittanceReturnsResult {returned, invoices[]}`). `InvoiceActions.markReturned`, all or none: every receipt's last SEPA attempt must be of this remittance (`400 VALIDATION_ERROR {field: invoiceIds}`), every receipt returnable as R-12-17 says (`409 INVALID_STATE {status}`), `at` not after today; each receipt then gets exactly what the single action gives (`FAILED{BANK_RETURN}` attempt appended, `FAILED`, `InvoiceFailed`, its `INVOICE_MARKED_FAILED` entry), its `InvoiceFailed` carrying `summarized: true`, plus one new `InvoicesFailed{remittanceId, invoiceIds, provider, reason}` (aggregate `Remittance`). `BillingNotificationFacts`: no N-10 from a `summarized` `InvoiceFailed`; one N-10 from `InvoicesFailed` with the existing variables (`member_name` the distinct names, `invoice_number` the numbers, both listed; `amount` the sum; `reason`); `NotificationCatalog` gives N-10 the second trigger. The single `markFailed` was split into `returnable`/`nextRun`/`failed`, shared with the bulk action, and gains `includeInNextRun` (also `InvoiceFailureRequest.includeInNextRun`, optional).
- (b) re-entry: `InvoiceState` gains `includeInNextRun` (null = unchanged; `transition` writes it only when set) and a failure sets it to the request's value (false when absent), so a re-entered receipt returned again waits for nothing unless the admin says so again. `InvoiceRepository.forNextRun` also returns `PERIODIC` `FAILED` `SEPA_DD` receipts with the flag and a positive total, so the simulation previews them, checks them against the member (`waitingReceiptIncident`) and the run presents them again as it does the waiting manual receipts (new `SEPA_XML` attempt, number + 1, the member's current mandate, `COLLECTING`, `InvoiceCollecting`). `includedIn(remittanceId, runId)` now returns every receipt of the remittance the run did not issue (manual or re-presented), so the rollback's blockers count them, and the rollback puts a re-presented periodic receipt back `FAILED` in its earlier remittance (its failure date and reason kept, the flag kept, a `FAILED{ROLLBACK}` attempt appended) where a manual one still goes back to `PENDING`.
- Tests added: unit `RemittanceSubmissionUndoTest` (5), `TargetCalendarTest` (16: Easter of six years, the closed days, eight margins, a negative one), `BillingExpirationsSurvivorsTest.SEPA_04_c_aSubmittedRemittanceIsSettledFiveTargetBusinessDaysAfterItsCollectionDate`, `InvoiceReturnsTest` (3), `BillingNotificationFactsReturnsTest` (2); ITs `RemittancesIT.SEPA_04_a_anAdminUndoesSentToTheBankBeforeTheCollectionDateAndTheRunCanBeRolledBack` (`@AuditCovers(REMITTANCE_SUBMISSION_UNDONE)`: 409 before submission, blank reason 400, undo with key replay, stored state, one audit entry, `submissionDue` and rollbackable again, refused on the collection date, then rolled back), `RemittancesIT.SEPA_04_a_aReceiptMarkedReturnedKeepsTheRemittanceSent`, `InvoiceActionsIT.SEPA_04_b_severalReturnsOfARemittanceInOneRequestGiveOneSummaryNotice` (a cash receipt and a future date refused with nothing moved, key replay, attempts, events, two audit entries, ONE N-10 naming both members and numbers with the summed amount, a repeat refused), `InvoiceActionsIT.SEPA_04_b_aReturnedPeriodicReceiptReEntersTheNextRemittanceAndARollbackPutsItBack` (manual receipt with the flag → 400; October's simulation previews it, its run presents it with attempt 2 and the current mandate, a return without the flag stays out; the rollback puts it back `FAILED` in September's remittance with the flag; presented again, then returned again without the flag it waits for nothing).
- Changed tests (asserting the decided behaviour or a new signature): `E8ScheduledProcessesIT.T_15_23_submittedSepaIsSettledOnCollectionDayOnce` → `T_15_23_SEPA_04_c_submittedSepaIsSettledFiveTargetBusinessDaysAfterTheCollectionDateOnce` (it settled on the collection date; now nothing on 01-09, 04-09 or 07-09, all four on 08-09, `paidAt` still 01-09); `BillingExpirationsSurvivorsTest` gets a `JobContext` with the parameter (0 keeps its four tests on the collection day); `RemittanceServiceSurvivorsTest`, `RemittancesControllerSurvivorsTest` (constructor arguments), `BillingRunServiceSurvivorsTest` (`includedIn` takes the run id); `E8ResponseContractTest` (event payloads: `InvoiceFailed` lists `summarized`, `InvoicesFailed` added with its fixture in `e8-events.json`, 34 events, 26 main rows); `E8ContractIT` (route counts 61 / 40 / 39) with the two routes in `e8-routes.json`; `handler-roles.txt` regenerated.
- Run and passing: unit `RemittanceSubmissionUndoTest`, `RemittanceServiceSurvivorsTest`, `AuditContractTest`, `TargetCalendarTest`, `BillingExpirationsSurvivorsTest`, `ParameterCatalogContractTest`, `E8ResponseContractTest`, `InvoiceReturnsTest`, `BillingNotificationFactsReturnsTest`, `InvoiceActionsSurvivorsTest`, `BillingRunServiceSurvivorsTest`, `RemittancesControllerSurvivorsTest`, `InvoicesControllerSurvivorsTest`, `NotificationCatalogContractTest`, `NotificationSpecTest`, `EventCatalogContractTest`, `BillingDocumentsSurvivorsTest`, `HandlerRolesSnapshotTest`; ITs after (a) `RemittancesIT` (18), `E8ContractIT` (74), `E2ContractIT` (8), `SecurityInventoryIT` (4), `OpenApiSnapshotTest` (12); after (c) on its own tree `E8ScheduledProcessesIT` (17), `SettingsIT` (23), `E2ContractIT` (8); after (b) `InvoiceActionsIT` (13), `RemittancesIT` (18), `E8ContractIT` (75), `BillingCycleIT` (22), `NotificationEngineIT` (15), `CardPaymentsIT` (120), `SecurityInventoryIT` (4), `OpenApiSnapshotTest` (12), `BillingFollowupsIT` (15), `InvoiceNumberingIT` (6), `PendingChargesIT` (4), `InvoiceConceptsIT` (4), `StoredRepresentationIT` (3), and `ArchitectureRulesTest`, `ArchitectureTest`, `HandlerRolesSnapshotTest`.
- Assumption: «before the collection date» is the club-local day strictly before `requestedCollectionDate`, the boundary R-12-14 already uses for `COLLECTION_DATE_PASSED`; «no invoice touched» is read on the remittance's own attempts: each receipt still `COLLECTING` in it, its attempt there open and the last one (a return, a payment by hand, a cancellation or a card charge all append an attempt or move the receipt).
- Assumption: the undo asks for a reason (as the rollback and the cancellation do) because the decision wants it audited; it changes no receipt and sends no notice (the submission sends none either).
- Assumption: re-entry is the admin's choice per return (`includeInNextRun`, as R-12-19 does for manual receipts), not automatic: a returned debit is re-presented only when the admin ticks it. Only a `PERIODIC` receipt may take it (the decision names periodic ones); a re-presented receipt keeps its number, period, lines and failure history and gets a new attempt with the member's current mandate.
- Assumption: the bulk route is scoped to one remittance («several invoices of a remittance»), all or none like the bulk payment; the summary is one N-10 with the code's existing variables and staff text (lists in `member_name` and `invoice_number`), so no new notification code or club template is needed; each receipt keeps its own `InvoiceFailed` (marked `summarized`) and audit entry.
- Assumption: the margin is counted in TARGET business days after the collection date (5 by default, 0 = the collection date), the parameter is named `billing.sepa.settlementBusinessDays`, CLUB-editable with bounds 0–40 (40 business days ≈ the 8-week refund period); `paidAt` stays the collection date (the day the money was collected), only the moment P5i records it moves.
- For the owner: (1) A re-presented receipt goes with the member's new monthly receipt in the same file: two debits for that member that month. Its `EndToEndId` is again its number, as for a manual receipt re-remitted after a rollback. (2) The run's audit `details.remittedManualInvoiceIds` and the rollback's `returnedManualInvoiceIds` now also list re-presented periodic receipts (keys unchanged). (3) With the margin, D6, `/me/invoices` and the accounting export show a sent remittance's receipts as collecting for a week longer; returns recorded in those days never become PAID. (4) `TargetCalendar` is the TARGET calendar SEPA-05 (p2-api-07) needs for the collection date. (5) The undo's check and its write are one Mongo transaction but read the receipts without locking them: an invoice action at the same instant could slip between them (the same exposure the rollback has).

- Review note (p2-api-03-sepa-high): for the owner. (1) Re-entry is chosen at the moment the return is recorded (`includeInNextRun` on the return). There is no way to flag a periodic receipt that is already `FAILED`: one returned before this change, or returned without the box ticked. Such a receipt can only be marked paid, or cancelled and replaced by a manual receipt with «next remittance». (2) The undo of «sent to the bank» does not lock the remittance's receipts (see point (5) above). A return or payment recorded at the same instant as an undo can leave a `GENERATED` remittance with one moved receipt; its rollback still lists that receipt as a blocker. (3) A re-presented debit keeps its receipt number as `EndToEndId` and the member's current mandate. Ask the bank whether it accepts the re-presentation in the same file as the member's new monthly debit.
### PAY-02 — FIXED
- Verified, no code touched. Any exception while a stored Stripe event is processed spends an attempt (`StripeWebhooks.java:74-78`). That includes the card read before the transaction (`:56-57`) and connection errors or 5xx, which `StripeCalls` does not treat as an outage. The cap is the club parameter `billing.stripeMaxAttempts`, which is defined for card retries (R-12-18; default 3, `min: 0`, `editableBy: CLUB`). At the cap the event is closed and its `work` payload unset (`PaymentRetryState.java:19-23`), about 30-50 s after receipt (10 s and 20 s backoff, `PaymentRecovery` every 10 s). A Stripe resend is then a duplicate and is ignored (`StripeWebhooks.java:43`), as R-12-21 states.
- To decide (R-12-21 only says "reintent intern"): (1) the webhook retry policy: its own constant or a PLATFORM parameter (closed catalogue), the backoff (minutes to hours) and the cap; (2) whether a provider outage (connection error, 5xx, 401/403/429) spends no attempt, as E8-T11 does for automatic refunds; (3) keep `work` on a FAILED event until the retention cleanup, and let a Stripe redelivery of a FAILED event re-queue it (this changes R-12-21's "duplicate → 200 without effect"); (4) how the admin learns of a FAILED event (N-55, a D6 item or an operator alert), and whether to build a daily reconciliation against Stripe Checkout Sessions and refunds.
- Meanwhile (NOT-CODE): during launch, compare the Stripe dashboard with the app daily and search the logs for `Stripe event recovery exhausted`. Keep `billing.stripeMaxAttempts` at 3 or more.

Pass 2 (owner decision of 13 Nov 2026): webhook processing retries with a growing back-off over about 24 hours, a constant and not a parameter; a provider outage (connection error, 5xx, 401/403/429) spends no attempt; a Stripe redelivery of an event recorded as FAILED re-queues it; FAILED events are kept until the retention cleanup and an ADMIN sees them in billing (API list route here, back-office view in the web).
- Retry policy: `StripeWebhooks.RETRY_BACKOFF` = 1 min, 5 min, 15 min, 30 min, 1 h, 2 h, 4 h, 8 h, 8 h (ten attempts over 23 h 51 min). `StripeInbox.failed(id, now, backoff)` replaces the shared `PaymentRetryState.failed` for webhook events: it spends an attempt (`outcome FAILED`), sets `nextAttemptAt` to that attempt's step, and after the tenth sets `processedAt` without unsetting `work`, so a stopped event keeps its allow-listed recovery data until `CleanupJob` deletes it (`jobs.retention.stripeEventsDays`, 400). The club parameter `billing.stripeMaxAttempts` is no longer read for webhooks; it still caps R-12-18's card retries and the payment operations (`PaymentRetryPolicy`, unchanged). The WARN `Stripe event recovery exhausted` is still written at the stop.
- Outages: `StripeCalls.outage(failure)` is true for a non-definitive `PaymentNotSubmitted` (what `StripeCalls` throws for Stripe's 401, 403 and 429, E8-T11) and for an `ApiException` caused by a Stripe connection error (status 0 in the SDK), a missing status or a 5xx. `StripeWebhooks.process` then calls `StripeInbox.postpone(id, now + 5 min)` (`OUTAGE_DEFERRAL` = `PaymentRefunds.DEFERRAL`): `outcome FAILED` and `nextAttemptAt`, attempts unchanged, so an outage never stops the event. `StripeWebhookReceived{FAILED}` is still published once.
- Redelivery: on a duplicate `eventId`, `StripeWebhooks.receive` calls `StripeInbox.requeue(id, work)`: only a row of the open club with `outcome FAILED` (still retried or stopped) goes back to a new receipt's state (`outcome`, `attempts`, `processedAt`, `nextAttemptAt` unset) with the delivery's allow-listed `work` (a row stopped by the old code had none), and is processed at once in the same request. Any other duplicate still has no effect. `receivedAt` and `payloadHash` stay the first delivery's.
- List: `GET /api/v1/billing/stripe-events` (`StripeEventsController.stripeEvents`; ADMIN without impersonation and module `BILLING`, as the other billing routes; read only, so no `Idempotency-Key` and no audit action). It is a universal list served by `StripeEventLists` (`stripe-events`, `BillingContractAccess.STRIPE_EVENTS`): the tenant `$match`, then `outcome = FAILED`; sort `receivedAt` (default newest first); no filter and no `q`; fields `id` (Stripe's `evt_…`), `type`, `receivedAt`, `attempts` (0 when only outages stopped it), `nextAttemptAt` (still retried), `processedAt` (stopped); never `work`. Schemas `StripeEventListItem` and `StripeEventPage`. Its errors (`400 INVALID_FILTER`, `404 MODULE_DISABLED`, the guards' 401/403) are all in the closed catalogue. The webhook's OpenAPI description now states the re-queue and the retry policy.
- Test support: `FakePaymentProvider.failCardRead(failure)` (local and test profiles only) makes the next card read of a webhook throw.
- Tests added: unit `StripeCallsTest.PAY_02_anOutageIsAConnectionErrorA5xxOrA401403429` (through the real `call` mapping); `StripeWebhooksSurvivorsTest.PAY_02_aFailedProcessingFollowsTheConstantBackoffOverAboutADayAndNeverTheClubsAttemptLimit`, `PAY_02_anOutageOfTheProviderSpendsNoAttempt`, `PAY_02_aRedeliveryIsProcessedOnlyWhenTheInboxRequeuedAFailedEvent`; `StripeInboxSurvivorsTest.PAY_02_theNthFailureWaitsTheNthStepOfTheBackoff`, `PAY_02_theFailureAfterTheLastStepStopsTheEventAndKeepsItsRecoveryData`, `PAY_02_aFailureOfAnEventAlreadyProcessedChangesNothing`, `PAY_02_anOutagePostponesTheEventWithoutSpendingAnAttempt`, `PAY_02_aRedeliveryRequeuesOnlyAFailedEventOfTheOpenClubWithTheDeliveredRecoveryData`. ITs `CardPaymentsIT.PAY_02_aProviderOutageSpendsNoAttemptAndTheEventIsProcessedOnceItPasses` (a paid checkout's card read meets a connection error, 503, 500, 401, 403 and 429 built by the real `StripeCalls`: no attempt, 5 min each, not run early, then PAID; a 404 refusal spends an attempt and waits 1 min), `PAY_02_aRedeliveryOfAFailedEventRequeuesItAndAnyOtherDuplicateHasNoEffect` (a row stopped by the old code, without `work`, is processed on redelivery; a processed duplicate and another club's FAILED row are untouched), `PAY_02_anAdminListsTheClubsFailedStripeEvents` (order, the three states, no `work`, `fields`, sort, the other club's own list, `filter`/`q` → 400, MEMBER → 403).
- Changed tests (they asserted the old policy or a signature): `CardPaymentsIT.T_12_15_recoveryIsFairBackedOffAndDeadLettersAfterConfiguredAttempts` → `T_12_15_PAY_02_recoveryIsFairBackedOffAndDeadLettersAfterAboutADay` (it asserted the stop after 3 attempts within two hours and the removal of `work`; now every step of the backoff, the stop after 10 attempts and `work` kept; the payment operations still stop at 3); `T_12_15_recoveryUsesTheClubsAttemptLimitAndRetainsItsDeadline` → `T_12_15_PAY_02_webhookRecoveryIgnoresTheClubsAttemptLimitAndRetainsItsDeadline` (it asserted the stop at the club's limit 2; now, with the limit at 1, the event runs again after 1 min and then waits 5 min); `StripeWebhooksSurvivorsTest` (two tests: the new `failed` signature, no `maxAttempts`); `StripeInboxSurvivorsTest.T_12_15_aFailedEventBelowTheMaximumIsRetried` (signature); `E8ContractIT` (route counts 62 / 41 / 40; the list's `x-filterable`, `x-sortable`, no `q`, the item requires only `id`) with the route in `e8-routes.json`; `ListFieldsContractIT` (the list in `OWN_LISTS`, two FAILED rows in its demo data); `handler-roles.txt`.
- Run and passing: unit `StripeCallsTest` (6), `StripeWebhooksSurvivorsTest` (12), `StripeInboxSurvivorsTest` (6), `HostedDemoStripeWebhooksTest` (1), then the whole unit suite after the last code change (509 classes, 5125 tests, 0 failures, 0 errors, 0 skipped; it includes `HandlerRolesSnapshotTest`, `ArchitectureTest`, `ArchitectureRulesTest`, `E8ResponseContractTest`); ITs `CardPaymentsIT` (123), `E8ContractIT` (76), `SecurityInventoryIT` (4), `OpenApiSnapshotTest` (12, after `bin/openapi-snapshot`), `ListFieldsContractIT` (4; its first run failed only because the demo data had no FAILED event to list, fixed with the two rows above), `CapturedPaymentInvariantsIT` (85), `PlatformConsoleIT` (43), `PaymentCurlIT` (1), `RemittancesIT` (18), `SingleClassCheckoutIT` (12), `E8PersistenceIT` (5).
- Assumption: «provider outage» is E8-T11's outage plus connection errors and 5xx. Every non-definitive `PaymentNotSubmitted` counts, so besides 401/403/429 a key the club stored but that cannot be used (decryption failure, mode mismatch) also spends no attempt: the same kind of failure, repaired outside the request. A disabled provider never reaches the card read (the registry answers no card). An outage never stops an event: it is retried every 5 minutes (E8-T11's deferral) until it is processed or the retention cleanup deletes it, and is listed as FAILED meanwhile.
- Assumption: the steps of the backoff (1 min to 8 h, ten attempts, 23 h 51 min). The first step is 1 min (it was 10 s), so an event that arrives before the one it depends on (a refund before the payment) still settles within a minute.
- Assumption: «re-queues it» is a fresh start: attempts back to 0, a new 24-hour schedule, processed at once in the delivery's request. It applies to a FAILED event still being retried as well as to a stopped one. The redelivered allow-listed data replaces the stored data (it is the same event; a row stopped before this change had none).
- Assumption: the list shows every FAILED event, retried or stopped (told apart by `nextAttemptAt` / `processedAt`), has no filter and no action. To process an event again, resend it from the Stripe dashboard (the decision names no action). No notification, dashboard item or reconciliation job was added: the decision names the billing list only.
- For the owner: (1) Events stopped by the old policy before this deploy (FAILED with `processedAt` and no `work`) appear in the list; resending each from the Stripe dashboard processes it. (2) An outage that never ends (a revoked key nobody replaces) keeps its events retried every 5 minutes until the 400-day retention: the list is where it shows. (3) `PaymentRetryState.failed` now serves only the payment operations; its `unset("work")` is dead for them and was left untouched.
- SPEC-CHANGE: docs/specs/S12-facturacio-i-pagaments.md R-12-21: the internal retry (constant backoff of about 24 h, ten attempts, not `billing.stripeMaxAttempts`), outages spend no attempt (5 min), a FAILED event is kept with its recovery data until the retention cleanup, a new delivery of a FAILED event re-queues it («duplicat → 200 sense efecte» no longer covers it), the ADMIN list; §3 `StripeEvent`: `attempts`, `nextAttemptAt?`, FAILED retried or stopped, recovery data kept; §6 new row `GET /billing/stripe-events`; §2 the D6 view; Canvis.
- SPEC-CHANGE: docs/specs/00-transversal/CONVENCIONS_API.md §3 S12 routes: `/billing/stripe-events`.
- SPEC-CHANGE: docs/openapi/openapi.json (regenerated): 1 operation, `StripeEventListItem`, `StripeEventPage`, the description of `POST /webhooks/stripe/{clubId}`; docs/openapi/CHANGELOG.md entry. Runbook: docs/DEPLOY.md, the Stripe providers paragraph. The closed catalogues are unchanged: no parameter (the policy is a constant), error code, event, notification or audit action was added; MATRIU_PERMISOS «Facturació» already gives ADMIN read.
- OTHER-REPO: web clubs-admin billing (D6): a read-only view «Esdeveniments Stripe fallits» (ADMIN; no mockup, design system) → `GET /api/v1/billing/stripe-events`, a universal list: `page`, `size` ∈ {20, 50, 200, 1000}, `sort=receivedAt,asc|desc` (default desc), `fields` ⊂ {id, type, receivedAt, attempts, nextAttemptAt, processedAt}; no `filter`, no `q` → `200 StripeEventPage {items[{id, type?, receivedAt?, attempts?, nextAttemptAt?, processedAt?}], page, size, totalItems, totalPages, appliedFilters}`; errors `400 INVALID_FILTER`, `403 FORBIDDEN` / `IMPERSONATION_DENIED`, `404 MODULE_DISABLED`. A row: the event id (copyable, to find it in Stripe), type, received, attempts, «es tornarà a provar el {nextAttemptAt}» or «aturat el {processedAt}»; a help line: resend a stopped event from the Stripe dashboard to process it again. Texts in ca/es/en; regenerate the API types from the snapshot.
- Review note: the implementation follows the decision; three effects have business meaning. (1) An outage (connection error, 5xx, 401/403/429, and also a disabled Stripe provider or a stored key that cannot be decrypted) never stops an event: it is retried every 5 minutes, with no cap, until it is processed or the 400-day retention deletes it. A revoked key that nobody replaces keeps the club's events FAILED in the list, with no other alert. (2) A stopped event now keeps its allow-listed recovery data (Stripe customer, payment method, payment intent and our memberId/invoiceId metadata, never the payload) for up to `jobs.retention.stripeEventsDays` (400 days); before, it was removed when the retries ran out. A redelivery brings its own copy of that data, so the decision would also be met by removing it at the stop. Keeping it follows the first pass's option (3). (3) The list also shows events that a short outage put back by 5 minutes, which clear on their own, so an admin may see FAILED rows that need no action.

### MIG-03 — NOT-CODE
- Run the production `--dry-run` with the candidate export several days before the window and clear every ERROR row: any ERROR blocks the whole plan (`MigrationStagedImporter.java:160-162`). For the whole apply, stop the core web container or close access at Caddy. Every production batch requires that no club document was written since the last one (`:250-257`), and the web container's outbox dispatcher (1 s poll) will likely claim the run's own `MigrationRunStarted`, which is itself a counted write. Rehearse the exact command sequence on a production copy and keep the approval file and run ids in the private runbook. After a dirty failure, a new approval can be limited to the unfinished stages; only stage 10 requires stages 2-9 (`:177-178`).

### MIG-06 — NOT-CODE
- Get real PlayOff exports of receipts, "Control packs", family groups and any mandates now, and write a `--mapping` for their real headings. The billing headings in `playoff-v2.yaml:121` are the rehearsal schema, and a header mismatch is an ERROR that blocks the whole plan, census included. Rehearse stages 2, 7 and 8 on a production copy. Load them with an approved reapply limited to those stages once PlayOff is frozen and its packs are final, because a loaded pack is never updated. Reconcile the report against PlayOff: open packs and remaining sessions, and the count and sum of PENDING/FAILED receipts. `--cut-over` must not precede PlayOff's last receipts, because later ones are skipped (`PlayoffBillingPlanner.java:55`).

### REL-01 — NOT-CODE
- Before L2, re-run the E11 gate on the exact launch commit: PIT (`./mvnw -q -Pmutation test-compile org.pitest:pitest-maven:mutationCoverage`, then `bin/mutation-gate`), compared with the 22 Oct scores (bookings 97.84 %, identity 95.71 %, payments 92.74 %); every `bin/e*-smoke` against the published image; the web `e2e:core` flows; and an independent review of `5fbcac58..<launch commit>` limited to payments, identity, bookings and census. Running PIT or the functional smokes in CI is possible, but CI time and secrets make it the owner's choice.

## api-03-authz-tenancy

### AUTHZ-02 — FIXED
- Verified, no code touched. For a non-ADMIN reader the members list adds `firstName`, `lastName1/2`, `contactEmails`, `phones`, `address` and `status` to the selectable fields (`CensusLists.java:82`). The dataset has no status scope (`CensusListProjection.java:24-30`), so LEFT members, including the imported ex-members, can be listed with `fields=` and `size=1000`. `GET /members/{id}` returns the e-mails, phones and address of any member who has not been erased to an INSTRUCTOR (`CensusQuery.java:34-41`). This is what the specs prescribe: `MATRIU_PERMISOS` row «Abonats» («R **tots** … dades de contacte visibles, IBAN no»), rule 1 «Instructor = visió global», and S03 R-03-31.
- To decide (a matrix and S03 change, plus `MemberInstructorView`, OpenAPI and the web instructor screens): (a) keep the global view; (b) limit the instructor's list and detail to ACTIVE (perhaps also PENDING) members, with 404 for a LEFT member's detail (check D13's history links first); (c) drop `address` from the instructor list and detail; (d) cap `size` or remove the `fields` projection for instructors, so the list cannot serve as an export.

Pass 2 (owner decision of 13 Nov 2026): instructors see only ACTIVE and PENDING members (no ex-members) and never the postal address; admin views are unchanged.
- List and facets: for a non-ADMIN reader `CensusListProjection.dataset` puts a first `$match {status: {$in: [ACTIVE, PENDING]}}` before every join (`CensusRules.INSTRUCTOR_VISIBLE_STATUSES`), so `GET /members`, its counts and `GET /members/filter-values` never reach an ex-member, a `q` or `status:eq:LEFT` included. The instructor's dataset no longer carries `address`, and `CensusLists` drops the `city` and `postalCode` columns and filters and the `city` sort for the instructor (asking for them is `400 INVALID_FILTER`, the existing answer for a key the role may not use). The ADMIN's dataset, columns, filters and sorts are unchanged (it keeps the optimised page selection; the instructor's list runs the full pipeline on the ACTIVE and PENDING rows only, as any scoped list does).
- Record: `CensusQuery.member(id, admin=false)` answers `404 NOT_FOUND` for a member whose status is not ACTIVE or PENDING (LEFT, migrated INACTIVE, erased) and leaves out `address`; the ADMIN view keeps its fields and their order. An ADMIN who is also an INSTRUCTOR keeps the ADMIN view (the existing role test).
- Contract: `MemberInstructorView` and `MemberInstructorListItem` lose `address`; `address` leaves the `x-fields` of `GET /members` and of `GET`/`POST /members/export` (it was the instructor's key only; the export is ADMIN-only and never accepted it); route descriptions say what an instructor gets. OpenAPI snapshot regenerated with `bin/openapi-snapshot`. No route, role, error code or catalogue entry added.
- Tests added: `CensusInstructorViewTest` (9 unit cases: the instructor's definition has no address column/filter/sort/field and the ADMIN's keeps them; the instructor's stages start with the status `$match` and project no address, the ADMIN's have no `$match`; the record is `NOT_FOUND` for LEFT and INACTIVE, and has no address for ACTIVE and PENDING; the status set); `CensusIT.AUTHZ_02_anInstructorReadsActiveAndPendingMembersWithoutTheirPostalAddress` (list, `totalItems`, `status:eq:LEFT`, `q`, status facets, the six refused address requests, records 200/404, the schema check, and the ADMIN still listing all six members with city and reading the LEFT record's address). `E2ResponseContractTest` and `E2ContractIT` now also forbid `address` in both instructor schemas.
- Tests changed to the decided behaviour: `ErasureIT.T_14_*` read the erased (LEFT) Montse as an INSTRUCTOR and expected 200 without `address`; it now expects `404 NOT_FOUND`. `UniversalListsIT.AUTHZ_01_instructorSearchMatchingOnlyAnIdNumberReturnsNoRows` ran on member-7, which is LEFT and therefore no longer listed to an instructor (its ID-number checks would have passed vacuously): it now runs the same checks on member-8 (ACTIVE) and adds that the instructor no longer finds member-7 by name while the ADMIN does. `e2-responses.json` fixture: `MemberInstructorView` without `address`.
- Run and passing: unit `CensusInstructorViewTest`, `CensusListsSearchTest`, `E2ResponseContractTest`, `ListItemCopiesContractTest`, `ArchitectureTest`, `HandlerRolesSnapshotTest`; ITs `CensusIT` (73), `ErasureIT` (21), `E2ContractIT` (8), `UniversalListsIT` (40), `ListFieldsContractIT` (4), `ExportEngineIT` (12), `OpenApiSnapshotTest` (12).
- Assumption: "the postal address" is the whole `address` object (street, postal code, city, province, country), so the instructor also loses the `city` and `postalCode` columns, filters and sort, which are its parts.
- Assumption: an ex-member is any status other than ACTIVE and PENDING (LEFT, and the INACTIVE status that migrated E1 records may still carry); an erased member is LEFT. A pending readmission is PENDING and stays visible, as the decision keeps PENDING.
- Assumption: only `/members` (list, facets, record) is scoped; the instructor's other reads that name a member without contact data (the dogs list and dog record `owner`, family groups, class and booking lists, D14) are unchanged.
- SPEC-CHANGE: docs/specs/S03-cens-abonats-gossos.md R-03-31: the instructor view has no postal address (`address`, and the `city`/`postalCode` columns, filters and sort answer `400 INVALID_FILTER`) and covers ACTIVE and PENDING members only (ex-members out of the list and facets, record `404`); admin views unchanged; example updated.
- SPEC-CHANGE: docs/specs/S03-cens-abonats-gossos.md T-03-33: also no `address`; a LEFT member is `404` and not counted by `GET /members`; Canvis line of 13-11-2026.
- SPEC-CHANGE: docs/specs/00-transversal/MATRIU_PERMISOS.md row «Abonats `/members`», INSTRUCTOR column: «R tots» → ACTIVE and PENDING members only, never ex-members, no IBAN and no postal address; rule 1 «Instructor = visió global» gains the same limit for `/members`.
- SPEC-CHANGE: docs/openapi/openapi.json: `MemberInstructorView` and `MemberInstructorListItem` without `address`; `address` out of the `x-fields` of `/members` and `/members/export`; descriptions of `GET /members`, `/members/filter-values`, `/members/{id}`.
- OTHER-REPO: web clubs-admin. Adopt the API snapshot (both instructor schemas lose `address`). `CensusListPage`: for an INSTRUCTOR do not offer the `city` and `postalCode` columns and filters nor the city sort (the API answers `400 INVALID_FILTER`), and drop them from a shared saved view before requesting it as an INSTRUCTOR. `CensusRecordPage`: an INSTRUCTOR opening an ex-member's record (a D13/D14 history link or an old URL) gets `404 NOT_FOUND`: show the existing not-found state, not an error. The status filter's values for an INSTRUCTOR are ACTIVE and PENDING only.

### AUTHZ-03 — FIXED
- New ArchUnit rule `ArchitectureRules.handlersDeclareTheirRoles` (`HANDLER_ROLES`), run by `ArchitectureTest.AUTHZ_03_everyHandlerDeclaresItsRolesOrIsAListedOpenRoute`: every `@RequestMapping`/`@Get…Mapping` method of a `@Controller`/`@RestController` must carry `@PreAuthorize` on the method or on its class, or be listed in `ArchitectureRules.OPEN_HANDLERS`. That list holds today's 31 open handlers, grouped by the chain that opens them: main-chain `permitAll` (health, branding, public pages, OIDC browser endpoints, magic link), the token chain, signed URLs and the `.ics` link, webhooks and the unsubscribe link, and the internal loopback chain. A listed entry that no longer names an unannotated handler also fails, so the list stays the inventory of open routes.
- The security chain is unchanged (`anyRequest().authenticated()`, `SecurityConfiguration.java:82`). Ending it with `denyAll()` would mean repeating every `permitAll` matcher, and the rule already closes the gap.
- Tests: `ArchitectureTest` (23 rules) and three new `ArchitectureRulesTest` cases ran and passed. The new cases compile synthetic controllers into a temporary directory. They check that an unannotated handler fails, as does a plain `@Controller` with `@RequestMapping`; that helper methods and non-controllers are ignored; that a rule on the method or on the class passes; and that a stale entry fails. As a check, removing the `@PreAuthorize` of `MembersController.getMember` made `ArchitectureTest` fail; that change was reverted. Step 3 of `docs/playbooks/new-endpoint.md` now names the rule.
- Not covered: the rule checks that a role rule exists, not that it matches `MATRIU_PERMISOS`. The per-role route fixtures (`e8-routes.json` and the other `e*-routes.json`) remain the matrix check.

### AUTHZ-04 — NOT-CODE
- Verified: `TenantRepository` binds `clubId` only in its own methods and exposes `mongo` to its 74 subclasses (`TenantRepository.java:16`). The list pipeline starts with a `clubId` `$match` (`MongoListRepository.java:249-251`), which the export stream relies on (`:66-75`), and each census `$lookup` carries its own (`CensusListProjection.java:209-216`). No query without the tenant was found in those paths. Before a second club is onboarded, review every query outside `TenantRepository`'s own methods and every `$lookup`, and add a cross-tenant 404 case per repository and endpoint, as `MATRIU_PERMISOS` rule 5 asks. None of this matters while there is a single tenant.

### AUTHZ-05 — FIXED
- Verified, no code touched. The support token carries `roles: [ADMIN]` (`SupportAccessService.java:78-82`). Only the `RESTRICTED` routes refuse it (`:51-58`, `CurrentUserFilter.java:68-76`): exports and their files, SEPA files, member impersonation, scheduling or cancelling an erasure, the account's own credentials, and ending a support access. Everything else a club ADMIN can do stays open to it, including the payment method, roles, billing runs, remittance submission, message sends and the full member record (ID document, internal notes). This is what S17 R-17-10 and the matrix row «Accés de suport» prescribe; R-17-12 names support access as the way in that rule 4 allows.
- Answers to the auditor's open points. Any active `AGILITYHUB_ADMIN` can start a grant for any club, giving only a `reason`; the club does not approve it (`start`, `:71-86`). The grant lasts `platform.supportAccessMinutes`: default 60, `min: 0`, no maximum, `editableBy: PLATFORM` (`catalog.yaml:1828-1837`), with no refresh. Every request re-checks the grant and the actor (`validate`, `:92-104`). The start is audited `SUPPORT_ACCESS_STARTED` in the club's audit, and the club ADMIN can end the access (`DELETE /support-accesses/{id}`). Audited actions carry `origin = PLATFORM_SUPPORT` (`AuditWriter.java:121`); writes that the specs do not audit leave no trace.
- To decide (an S17 change): (a) keep full ADMIN with today's exclusions; (b) make support read-only on census and money, by extending `RESTRICTED` and `@SupportRestricted` and keeping `SecurityInventoryIT` in step; (c) require a club ADMIN's approval for each grant (new state, screen and notice); (d) hide `idDocument` and `internalNotes` from support reads. Separately, set a maximum for `platform.supportAccessMinutes`.
- Review note: S17 R-17-10 reads «**totes** les accions auditades amb `origin = PLATFORM_SUPPORT`» and, in round 2, «cada acció feta amb el token s'audita amb `actorRole = PLATFORM`», but `AuditWriter.java:120-121` only tags the actions that are already `@Audited`; a support write whose service method carries no `@Audited` leaves no audit entry. Decide whether R-17-10 means every support request must be audited (new audit action in the S14 catalogue) or only the audited ones, and align the spec wording either way.

Pass 2 (owner decision of 13 Nov 2026): support access keeps today's permissions; `platform.supportAccessMinutes` gets a maximum in the catalogue; every write made with a support token leaves an audit entry with origin `PLATFORM_SUPPORT`, also writes whose service carries no `@Audited` (R-17-10 as written).
- Maximum (commit 36ec8e0): `catalog.yaml` `platform.supportAccessMinutes` `constraints {min: 0, max: 60}` (default 60, `editableBy: PLATFORM` unchanged). The existing parameter machinery applies it: `PUT /platform/clubs/{id}/parameters/platform.supportAccessMinutes` above 60 → `400 PARAMETER_INVALID`; an override already stored above it is ignored (`ParameterInvalidOverride`), so a grant gets the product's 60 minutes. Permissions (`SupportAccessService.RESTRICTED`, `@SupportRestricted`) untouched.
- Every support write: new `AuditAction.SUPPORT_WRITE` (also in `AuditContracts.AuditActionName`, so the OpenAPI `AuditAction` enum and the `action` filter of `GET /audit-entries` know it), an «event» action in `AuditWriter.EVENT_ACTIONS` (no `changes`). `shared.api.SupportWriteAuditInterceptor` (registered in `ModuleConfiguration` after the support-restricted and module interceptors) notes the start of each `POST`/`PUT`/`PATCH`/`DELETE` of a validated support token (`CurrentUser.support()`), skipping async/error dispatches and the replay of an `Idempotency-Key` (`IdempotentReplay` pending: its handler does not run). After the handler, when the answer is 2xx and no exception escaped, it calls the port `shared.application.SupportWriteAuditor`, implemented by `platform.application.audit.SupportWriteAudit`: if the club's audit holds no entry of the request (`traceId` of the request, `origin = PLATFORM_SUPPORT`, `at` since the start: `AuditRepository.supportEntrySince`, on the `audit_club_at` index), it writes `SUPPORT_WRITE` with `entityType = ImpersonationGrant`, `entityId` = the grant, `details {method, route (the matched pattern), path, status}`; actor, `actorRole = PLATFORM`, `support = true` and the origin come from `AuditWriter` as for any support entry. A write its service audits keeps that entry alone. A failure to write the entry is logged (`SupportWriteAuditFailed`, grant, method and path only) and never turns the answered write into an error. The port keeps `shared` free of the platform context (ArchUnit) and lets the interceptor sit beside `SupportRestrictedInterceptor`, where it can see the replay. The console's audit (`/platform/audit-entries`) does not list it (R-17-12: its query names only the support start and end).
