### COMM-07 — FIXED
- Verified, no code touched. `BrevoEmailSender.java:54` turns any answer other than 201 into `Brevo HTTP nnn`, and `NotificationDispatcher.emailRetryable` (:265-273) retries only 429 and 5xx, as R-11-09 prescribes, for at most 5 attempts over 81 minutes (`RetryPolicy`). An announcement is one `AnnouncementSent` (`AnnouncementService.java:85-101`), whose deliveries the poll sends up to 200 at a time (`POLL_BATCH`, :100) every 5 s. Failed deliveries cannot be resent, and nothing outside messaging consumes `NotificationFailed`.
- To decide: (a) which Brevo answers mean "allowance spent", and whether they become retryable with a back-off longer than R-11-09's last step (an R-11-09 change); (b) a send rate for announcements (a value tied to the Brevo plan); (c) a "resend failed deliveries" action per batch on the announcement log (screen, role, audit action in the closed catalogue); (d) an admin notice when a batch has failures (a new catalogue notification).
- NOT-CODE: confirm the Brevo plan's daily allowance against about 190 members with up to two addresses each, and the status Brevo returns once it is spent.

Pass 2 (owner decision of 13 Nov 2026): announcements (mass e-mail) are throttled to the provider plan (a rate constant or parameter); provider quota errors (429 / quota 4xx) are retried later instead of being final; the back office gets a «resend failed» action for an announcement.
- Pace: new `AnnouncementEmailPace` (messaging.application.engine): at most `ANNOUNCEMENT_EMAILS_PER_MINUTE` announcement e-mails (code N-24, which the `CUSTOM` templates are sent as) per clock minute, platform-wide and in this process. `NotificationDispatcher.sendEmail` asks it after the existing checks (disabled delivery, `deliverable`, suppression) and before rendering; one beyond the pace takes COMM-06's `Outcome.deferred`: `QUEUED` until the next minute with `lastError` «Announcement e-mail pace», no attempt counted, nothing sent or published. Other e-mails are never paced. The dispatcher gains a constructor with the pace (the previous one means no pace, as the tests that build their own use it); `MessagingConfiguration` passes `notifications.announcements.emails-per-minute` (`application.yml`, env `ANNOUNCEMENT_EMAILS_PER_MINUTE`, default 60; below 1 the API refuses to start), listed in `.env.example`, `deploy/compose.prod.yaml` and docs/DEPLOY.md's inventory.
- Quota: `NotificationDispatcher.emailQuota` recognises 429, 402 and Brevo's `not_enough_credits` (whatever the status); such an answer is retryable (`emailRetryable`) and `failed(...)` schedules it with the new `RetryPolicy.nextQuotaAttempt`: 1, 5, 15, 60 minutes, then every 60 minutes, final (`FAILED` + `NotificationFailed`) at the 28th failing attempt, about 24 hours after the first. `BrevoEmailSender` reads the `code` of a 4xx's small JSON body and appends ` not_enough_credits` to «Brevo HTTP nnn» when it is that code; nothing else of a body is ever read. Any other 4xx is still final at once; SMS is unchanged.
- Resend: new `POST /api/v1/announcements/{batchId}/resend-failed` in `MessageTemplatesController` (ADMIN, impersonation refused, by the class's rule; `Idempotency-Key` required like the send; no body; `202 AnnouncementResendResult {batchId, resent}`; errors `VALIDATION_ERROR`, `NOT_FOUND`, `IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_REUSED`) → new `AnnouncementResends.resendFailed`, one transaction: the club's batch (`AnnouncementRepository`, tenant-scoped: another club's or an unknown one is `NOT_FOUND`), then `NotificationRepository.requeueFailedAnnouncementEmails`, which puts every `FAILED` EMAIL delivery of the batch's notices (`eventType` `AnnouncementSent`, `dedupKey` `{batchId}:…`) back to `QUEUED`, due now, attempts 0, `failedAt` removed, last error kept until the next attempt (one `updateMulti` with an array filter), and the new audit action `ANNOUNCEMENT_RESENT` on the batch's template, no changes, `details {batchId, resent}`, written only when at least one was put back. The 5-second poll sends them at the pace with every check of a send (a suppressed address ends `SKIPPED_NO_CONTACT`). `ANNOUNCEMENT_RESENT` is in `AuditAction`, `AuditContracts.AuditActionName` (the OpenAPI enum) and `AuditWriter.EVENT_ACTIONS`.
- Tests added: unit `AnnouncementPaceAndQuotaTest` (3: the pace per clock minute and the next minute, the setting's floor; which answers are quota, retryable or final; the quota schedule and its ~24 h total, R-11-09's 5 attempts kept for the others), `BrevoEmailSenderTest.T_11_09_COMM_07_aSpentAllowanceIsToldApartByItsCodeOnly` (400, 402, 403 with `not_enough_credits` and with another code); ITs `NotificationDispatcherIT.T_11_09_COMM_07_aQuotaAnswerIsRetriedForAboutADayInsteadOfBeingFinal` (429, 402 and `not_enough_credits` answers through 27 attempts still `QUEUED` at the right times, no `NotificationFailed`, the 28th final; a plain 400 final at once) and `T_11_18_COMM_07_announcementEmailsGoAtTheProvidersPaceAndOtherEmailsNeverWait` (a pace of 2: two of an announcement's four e-mails go, two wait with no attempt, an N-09 of the same minute goes at once, the two go at the next minute), `AnnouncementsIT.T_11_18_COMM_07_resendFailedQueuesTheBatchsFailedEmailsAgainAndAuditsIt` (`@AuditCovers(ANNOUNCEMENT_RESENT)`: 403 for MEMBER and INSTRUCTOR, 404 for another club and an unknown batch, 202 `{resent: 1}` with only that batch's failed e-mail requeued — another batch's failed e-mail and a failed push untouched —, the audit entry read through `GET /audit-entries`, the same key replaying the same answer with one entry, the dispatcher sending it, `resent: 0` writing nothing). Each fails without the change.
- Changed tests: `E7ContractIT` (route fixture `contracts/e7-routes.json` gains the route, ADMIN, idempotent, 202, resource: a stored batch `e7-batch-a`, `announcements` cleaned; 22 → 23 routes; the idempotent routes are the send and the resend); `snapshots/security/handler-roles.txt` (+1 line, regenerated with `-Dsnapshots.update=true`).
- Run and passing: unit, every unit test class under `clubs/messaging`, `configuration`, `platform/application/audit` and `arch`, plus `ParameterCatalogContractTest` (95 classes, 3,352 tests), among them `AnnouncementPaceAndQuotaTest` (3), `BrevoEmailSenderTest` (15), `HandlerRolesSnapshotTest`, `RecentLoginRoutesTest`, `AuditContractTest`, `E7ResponseContractTest` (5); ITs `NotificationDispatcherIT` (28), `AnnouncementsIT` (6), `E7ContractIT` (30), `OpenApiSnapshotTest` (12, after `bin/openapi-snapshot`), `SecurityInventoryIT` (4), `E2ContractIT` (8, S14's action list against the contract enum), `IdempotentReplayContractIT` (2). Note for the reviewer: run in one JVM after `AnnouncementsIT`, `NotificationDispatcherIT.R_11_06_theIntentsWrittenBeforeTheEngineAreClosedByTheMigrationAndNeverSent` errors with a NullPointerException in `LegacyNotificationRows.upgrade`: the migration reads every club's rows without `deliveries`, and `AnnouncementsIT.T_11_26_INC_53_…` leaves one without `status`. A test-isolation defect that predates this change (neither test is changed by it); each class passes on its own and in other orders.
- Assumption: the «rate constant/parameter» is a platform setting (environment variable), not a club parameter: the provider plan is the one account all clubs share, so a club must not raise it. Per clock minute and kept in memory (one API instance, ADR-003; a restart begins a fresh minute). Default 60 a minute: a Cànic announcement (about 190 members, up to two addresses each) takes some 7 minutes. A daily allowance smaller than an announcement is handled by the quota retries, not by the pace.
- Assumption: «quota 4xx» are 402 and Brevo's `not_enough_credits` code on any 4xx; 429 joins them. «Retried later» is about a day (a daily allowance renews within it), counted in attempts so that a resend starts again; it applies to every e-mail the engine sends (the allowance is the account's, and a transactional notice is as stuck as an announcement), not to SMS. An ordinary transient failure after a long quota wait is final once the attempt count is past R-11-09's five.
- Assumption: «resend failed» puts back only e-mail deliveries that ended `FAILED` (an SMS or push of a `CUSTOM` CLUB_CHANGES announcement is not resent); they keep their address and member (a member who left, or whose notice no longer applies, ends `SKIPPED_STALE`; a bounced address `SKIPPED_NO_CONTACT`). It answers 202 with the number put back, also 0; it publishes no event and is audited on the template, like the send. No admin notice when a batch has failures (pass 1's option (d) was not decided).
- For the owner: confirm the Brevo plan and set `ANNOUNCEMENT_EMAILS_PER_MINUTE` from it; a daily allowance is shared with sign-in links and every other notice, so it must cover the largest announcement plus a day's mail (written in docs/DEPLOY.md).
- SPEC-CHANGE: docs/specs/S11-comunicacions.md R-11-09 (quota answers retried 1 · 5 · 15 · 60 min then hourly, final at the 28th attempt; other 4xx final); R-11-13 (the pace `ANNOUNCEMENT_EMAILS_PER_MINUTE`; «Reenvia els fallits» and its audit); §6 new row `POST /announcements/{batchId}/resend-failed`; T-11-09 and T-11-18 (the COMM-07 cases); Canvis.
- SPEC-CHANGE: docs/specs/S14-tauler-auditoria-exportacions-rgpd.md R-14-09 (new `ANNOUNCEMENT_RESENT`), R-14-10 (an «event» action with `details {batchId, resent}`), Canvis.
- SPEC-CHANGE: docs/specs/00-transversal/MATRIU_PERMISOS.md «Comunicats massius» row (ADMIN X also on `/announcements/{batchId}/resend-failed`); docs/specs/00-transversal/CONVENCIONS_API.md S11 route list.
- SPEC-CHANGE: docs/openapi/openapi.json (regenerated: the operation, schema `AnnouncementResendResult`, `AuditAction` value `ANNOUNCEMENT_RESENT`); docs/openapi/CHANGELOG.md entry; docs/DEPLOY.md (inventory row, «Notifications» paragraph); `.env.example`; `deploy/compose.prod.yaml`.
- OTHER-REPO: web clubs-admin, after regenerating the API types from the snapshot: an action «Reenvia els fallits» / «Reenviar los fallidos» / «Resend failed» for an announcement batch, where the admin reads a batch's rows (the notification log filtered to code N-24, whose rows carry `batchId`; R-11-10): shown when the batch has an EMAIL delivery `FAILED`, with a confirmation that names how many («Es tornaran a enviar {n} correus que han fallat») and its own confirm button; it calls `POST /api/v1/announcements/{batchId}/resend-failed` with a fresh `Idempotency-Key` (UUID) and no body, roles ADMIN (not under impersonation); `202 {batchId, resent}` → a toast «{resent} correus tornen a la cua» (0: «No hi ha cap correu fallit per reenviar») and a refetch of the log; `404 NOT_FOUND` → «Aquest comunicat ja no existeix»; `409 IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_REUSED` only on a reused key. Texts in ca/es/en, keyboard and screen-reader access as the log's other actions, a unit test of the dialog and both answers. Audit labels for the new action `ANNOUNCEMENT_RESENT` («Comunicat: correus fallits reenviats» / «Comunicado: correos fallidos reenviados» / «Announcement: failed e-mails resent») where the audit list names the actions, with `details.batchId` and `details.resent`. The log shows a waiting e-mail's `lastError` «Announcement e-mail pace» or «SMS quiet hours» as it shows any other; no other web change.
- Review note: reviewed b041676 against the decision (the pace, the quota answers and their schedule, `BrevoEmailSender`'s body read, the route, its role and impersonation rule, tenancy, idempotency, audit, the catalogues, the spec copies and the OpenAPI snapshot): it implements the three parts and nothing beyond. Correction (review commit): `requeueFailedAnnouncementEmails` also put back a `FAILED` e-mail whose target an erasure had removed (S14 R-14-15 unsets `deliveries.$[].target`); the poll never claims a delivery without a target, so that row stayed `QUEUED` for ever and was counted in `resent`. Now only a failed e-mail with a target is put back and counted; `AnnouncementsIT.T_11_18_COMM_07_…` gains the case (it fails without the fix). For the owner: the Assumption above that «a member who left, or whose notice no longer applies, ends `SKIPPED_STALE`» does not hold: an announcement has no staleness check (`MessagingNotificationFacts` keeps the default `deliverable = true`) and preferences are applied only when the notice is created, so a resend (and a quota retry, which can now run for about 24 h) still writes to a member who left the club or turned that e-mail off, or unsubscribed from club news, after the original send; the `AnnouncementResends` Javadoc now says so. Rechecking status and preference at each attempt would be a separate change. Run here: unit `AnnouncementPaceAndQuotaTest`, `BrevoEmailSenderTest`, `AuditContractTest`, `HandlerRolesSnapshotTest`, `E7ResponseContractTest`, `RecentLoginRoutesTest` and the messaging contract tests, all passing; ITs `AnnouncementsIT` (6), `NotificationDispatcherIT` (28, alone; in one JVM after `AnnouncementsIT` its `R_11_06_theIntentsWrittenBeforeTheEngine…` errors with the pre-existing `LegacyNotificationRows` NullPointerException the entry describes — both tests come from the production base), `E7ContractIT`, `SecurityInventoryIT`, `OpenApiSnapshotTest`, `E2ContractIT`, `IdempotentReplayContractIT`.

### COMM-08 — FIXED
- Verified, no code touched. CLUB_NEWS mail carries only `List-Unsubscribe: <{club app}/comunicats/baixa?t=…>` (`NotificationEmailRenderer.java:62-63`; URL built in `NotificationDispatcher.sendEmail`), which is what S11 R-11-08 prescribes (organizer 28-09, E69: the link opens the club app's page, which sends the token to `POST /email-unsubscribes {token}`). The token is HMAC-SHA256, bound to club and member, valid 30 days, and only turns CLUB_NEWS e-mail off (`UnsubscribeTokens.java:34-53`, `EmailUnsubscribeService.java:33-41`).
- To decide (an R-11-08 and §6 change, and the OpenAPI contract): RFC 8058 one-click needs `List-Unsubscribe-Post: List-Unsubscribe=One-Click` and an HTTPS URL that takes the provider's form `POST` with the token in the URL itself. Options: (a) a new anonymous core route (for example `POST /api/v1/email-unsubscribes/one-click?t=…`, rate-limited like the existing one) listed in the header, the footer link still opening the page; (b) keep the page only and go without the Gmail/Yahoo one-click button.
- OTHER-REPO (no API change, consistent with R-11-08): web `apps/clubs/src/UnsubscribePage.tsx:39-45` should send the token when the member presses a button, not in a `useEffect` on load, so link scanners and forwarded copies cannot unsubscribe a member.

Pass 2 (owner decision of 13 Nov 2026): RFC 8058 one-click unsubscribe for CLUB_NEWS e-mail (`List-Unsubscribe-Post: List-Unsubscribe=One-Click` and an HTTPS URL that takes the provider's POST with the token, a new anonymous route), and the unsubscribe page asks for a button press instead of unsubscribing on load.
- New route `POST /api/v1/email-unsubscribes/one-click?t={token}` (`EmailUnsubscribesController.unsubscribeEmailOneClick`): anonymous like its neighbour `POST /email-unsubscribes` (`security: []`, `permitAll` in `SecurityConfiguration`, listed in `ArchitectureRules.OPEN_HANDLERS`), the club from the host (`TenantFilter`'s public routes), the same anonymous rate-limit family (`RateLimitFilter`), not idempotency-keyed (as its neighbour). The body must be the form field `List-Unsubscribe=One-Click` (`application/x-www-form-urlencoded` or `multipart/form-data`); then `EmailUnsubscribeService.unsubscribe(token)` runs exactly as for the page's call (CLUB_NEWS e-mail off, one `EmailUnsubscribed`, a repeat changes nothing; no audit action, as the existing route has none). Errors from the closed catalogue only: another body or no/blank `t` → `400 VALIDATION_ERROR` (`fieldErrors`), an invalid token → `422 UNSUBSCRIBE_TOKEN_INVALID`. There is no GET (an anonymous GET of the URL is 401 and changes nothing). New form schema `EmailOneClickUnsubscribeForm`.
- Mail: `NotificationEmailRenderer` takes `Unsubscribe(page, oneClick)` (built by `Unsubscribe.of(origin, token)` with one signed token) instead of the page URL; on `CLUB_NEWS` only, the headers are `List-Unsubscribe: <https://{club app host}/api/v1/email-unsubscribes/one-click?t=…>` and `List-Unsubscribe-Post: List-Unsubscribe=One-Click` (the page is no longer in the header: RFC 8058 wants one HTTPS URI there), and the footer link keeps the page `/comunicats/baixa?t=…`. `NotificationDispatcher.sendEmail` and the D9 preview (`TemplatePreviewService`) build both. The club host already proxies `/api/*` to the API (`deploy/Caddyfile`, `api_proxy`), so no proxy change.
- Tests added: IT `EmailUnsubscribeIT.T_11_23_COMM_08_theOneClickPostOfTheListUnsubscribeUrlTurnsClubNewsOffOnce` (form-encoded POST → 200 `{category: CLUB_NEWS}`, CLUB_NEWS off and the other preferences kept, one event; a multipart repeat and the page's call publish nothing more; a multipart POST unsubscribes another member on its own) and `…_theOneClickUrlRefusesAnythingButTheProvidersPostOfAValidToken` (anonymous GET 401; no body, empty, `one-click`, `Unsubscribe` → 400; no or blank `t` → 400; tampered, another club's, unknown member's, the token on another club's host, expired → 422; unknown host → 404 `UNKNOWN_HOST`; nothing written, no event). Both fail without the route.
- Changed tests (they asserted the old header): `NotificationEmailRendererTest.T_11_23_clubNewsCarriesTheUnsubscribeFooterAndHeaderAndTransactionalMailsNever` (the header is the one-click URL plus `List-Unsubscribe-Post`, the footer the page; an `http` origin writes neither) and `NotificationEngineIT.T_11_23_clubNewsMailsCarryTheSignedUnsubscribeLinkAndTransactionalOnesNever` (the header's URL is `/api/v1/email-unsubscribes/one-click?t=`, `List-Unsubscribe-Post` present, the footer is the page with the same token); `NotificationSeedSnapshotTest` passes the two URLs. Route fixtures: `contracts/e7-routes.json` gains the route (ANON + every role, `scope: PUBLIC`, params `t` and `List-Unsubscribe`, not idempotent); `E7ContractIT` counts 24 routes, both unsubscribe routes answer the fixture's token with 422 and are the only ANON ones, and the operation publishes `t` in the query and the two form media types; `AnonymousRateLimitsTest` lists the path; `snapshots/security/handler-roles.txt` +1 line (regenerated with `-Dsnapshots.update=true`).
- Run and passing: unit `NotificationEmailRendererTest` (4), `NotificationSeedSnapshotTest` (3), `MessagingE7T03UnitTest` (7), `E7ResponseContractTest` (5), `AnonymousRateLimitsTest` (34), `HandlerRolesSnapshotTest`, `ArchitectureTest` (23), `ArchitectureRulesTest` (23), `RecentLoginRoutesTest`; `OpenApiSnapshotTest` (12, after `bin/openapi-snapshot`); ITs `EmailUnsubscribeIT` (4), `E7ContractIT` (31), `SecurityInventoryIT` (4: the new operation is anonymous, not 401, and has a rate-limit route), `SecurityHardeningIT` (5), `MessageTemplatesIT` (11), `NotificationEngineIT` (16; its `T_11_31_aHundredSimultaneousReminders…` errored once with a Mongo connection-pool timeout while the machine's load was at 9, in one JVM with two other classes, and passed on the re-run alone: an environment timeout of a 100-thread test, not this change).
- Assumption: «accepts the provider's POST» is RFC 8058 §3.2 read strictly: only a POST whose form body is exactly `List-Unsubscribe=One-Click` unsubscribes (any other body, a JSON one included, is 400), in either of the two encodings the RFC allows; the token travels in the URL's query as `t` (the same signed 30-day token as the page's link), and the answer is the neighbour's `200 EmailUnsubscribeResult`.
- Assumption: the header carries only the one-click HTTPS URL (RFC 8058 §3.1: one HTTPS URI); no `mailto:` is added (the platform has no unsubscribe mailbox). The header is on CLUB_NEWS mail only, as before (R-11-08: transactional mail never carries it). A club without a verified app domain still gets neither the header nor the footer, as before.
- For the owner: Gmail and Yahoo show their one-click «Unsubscribe» only on DKIM-signed mail whose signature covers both headers; check the sending domain's DKIM in Brevo and send a test announcement to a Gmail address (written in docs/DEPLOY.md, «Notifications»).
- SPEC-CHANGE: docs/specs/S11-comunicacions.md R-11-08 (the one-click header and route, its answers, no GET; the page asks for a button press); §6 new row `POST /email-unsubscribes/one-click?t=`; T-11-23 (the COMM-08 cases); Canvis.
- SPEC-CHANGE: docs/specs/00-transversal/CONVENCIONS_API.md S11 route list (`/email-unsubscribes/one-click`). No catalogue change: no error code, event, notification, parameter or audit action added (the new route reuses `VALIDATION_ERROR`, `UNSUBSCRIBE_TOKEN_INVALID`, `EmailUnsubscribed`). MATRIU_PERMISOS has no row for the anonymous unsubscribe and is unchanged.
- SPEC-CHANGE: docs/openapi/openapi.json (regenerated: the operation and schema `EmailOneClickUnsubscribeForm`); docs/openapi/CHANGELOG.md entry; docs/DEPLOY.md «Notifications» (the header, DKIM check).
- OTHER-REPO: web `apps/clubs/src/UnsubscribePage.tsx` (route `/comunicats/baixa?t=…`): stop sending the token in the `useEffect` on load (`:39-45`); show the page with a short explanation («Deixaràs de rebre els comunicats del club per correu.» / «Dejarás de recibir los comunicados del club por correo.» / «You will stop receiving the club's announcements by e-mail.») and a primary button «Deixa de rebre'ls» / «Darme de baja» / «Unsubscribe» that calls the unchanged `POST /api/v1/email-unsubscribes {token}` once (disabled while working); keep today's outcomes (success, `422`/`400` → invalid link, other errors → retry) and the link to the preferences; no token in the URL → the invalid-link state without a button. Texts in ca/es/en under `notifications:unsubscribe.*`, the button reachable by keyboard with the page's existing `aria-busy`/live-region behaviour, and a unit test that nothing is sent on load and one call goes on the press. The new one-click route is for mailbox providers only: the web never calls it.
- Review note (review of p2-api-12-messaging-jobs, 14 Nov 2026): accepted; the route, its anonymity, host tenancy, rate-limit family, errors, fixtures and OpenAPI snapshot check out, and the ITs pass (`EmailUnsubscribeIT` 4, `E7ContractIT` 31, `SecurityInventoryIT` 4, `SecurityHardeningIT` 5, `NotificationEngineIT` 16, `OpenApiSnapshotTest` 12). Three things the owner should know. (1) The `List-Unsubscribe` header now names the API's POST-only URL instead of the page. A mail client that does not do RFC 8058 and simply opens that URL in a browser (a GET) shows the member a raw `401` JSON error and does not unsubscribe them. Gmail and Yahoo POST, and the footer link still opens the page. If that is not acceptable, a GET of `/api/v1/email-unsubscribes/one-click?t=…` could answer `303` to the page `/comunicats/baixa?t=…`. That would be a new anonymous GET: it needs its own decision, plus fixtures and OpenAPI, and it must never unsubscribe on GET. (2) The token now reaches the API in the query string `t`. Access logging is off (docs/DEPLOY.md). If it is ever switched on, the Caddyfile's `access_log` snippet deletes only `access_token`, so 30-day unsubscribe tokens would be logged. Add `delete t` there before enabling it. (3) The second half of the decision (the page asks for a button press) is only in the web repository (OTHER-REPO above). Until that ships, the page still unsubscribes on load.

### COMM-09 — NOT-CODE

### COMM-10 — FIXED
- Verified, no code touched. The push payload carries the notification's full title and body (`NotificationDispatcher.java:298-300`), which web `sw.ts:75-85` shows as is; the TTL is the one club-wide `messaging.push.ttlMinutes`, 1440 (`catalog.yaml:1304-1306`), as R-11-07 prescribes; EXPIRED subscriptions are deleted only by an account's erasure (`MessagingErasureRepository.java:43-44`).
- To decide: (a) a TTL per code (N-13 never beyond the class start, N-15 a few minutes or until `confirm_by`), as constants or new catalogue parameters (an R-11-07 change); (b) how long EXPIRED subscriptions are kept (for example 30 or 90 days), and which job purges them (S14's retention sweep or a new one), to record in the processing register; (c) whether lock screens show a generic text instead of the body for some codes (announcements).

Pass 2 (owner decision of 13 Nov 2026): push TTL per code — a class reminder is never delivered after the class start, a seat offer lives until its confirm-by deadline — and EXPIRED push subscriptions are purged after 90 days by the retention job.
- TTL: new `PushTtl` (messaging.domain) — N-13: the club's `messaging.push.ttlMinutes` cut at the start (the smaller of the two); N-15 with a deadline: exactly until it, also beyond the club's TTL; any other code, or no deadline (an ALL_AT_ONCE offer): the club's TTL; a deadline now or past: nothing to send. The deadline is the event owner's, through a new default hook `NotificationFactsPort.pushDeadline(StoredNotification)` (empty by default), read at every attempt: `BookingNotificationFacts` answers the class booking's `classStartsAt` for N-13 and the waitlist entry's `confirmBy` for N-15 (null in ALL_AT_ONCE → none); `TrainingNotificationFacts` the training booking's `startsAt` for a training N-13. `NotificationDispatcher.sendPush` takes the earliest deadline of the event's owners: past it, the delivery ends `SKIPPED_STALE` with `lastError` «Push deadline passed» and the push service is not called (a reminder retried after the class start included); otherwise `PushPayload` carries the TTL (new optional `ttlSeconds`, never part of the encrypted payload; a six-argument constructor kept) and `WebPushSender` sends it as the `TTL` header, the club's value staying its fallback.
- Purge: new `PushSubscriptionRetention` (messaging.application), one more `RetentionTask` of the monthly `retention-sweep` (S14 R-14-16): items `PushSubscription` / `PURGE_EXPIRED_PUSH_SUBSCRIPTION`, counter `expiredPushSubscriptionsPurged`, for the club's subscriptions `EXPIRED` with `expiredAt` at or before the run's `scheduledFor` minus 90 days (`PushSubscriptionRepository.expiredBefore`); the delete (`deleteExpired`) matches the same condition again, so one the browser renewed meanwhile (`ACTIVE`, `expiredAt` unset) stays. PRIV-02's dry run and confirmation cover it like the other tasks (`WOULD_PURGE_EXPIRED_PUSH_SUBSCRIPTION`).
- Tests added: unit `PushTtlTest` (2: the reminder cut and its edges, a shorter club TTL, the seat offer beyond the club's TTL, no deadline, other codes, a negative or missing club value), `PushDeadlineFactsTest` (2: S08's class start and `confirmBy`, none for ALL_AT_ONCE, another code, a training subject or a booking that is gone; S09's training start), `WebPushSenderTest.T_11_10_COMM_10_thePayloadsOwnTtlIsTheRequestsTtl` (the payload's TTL is the request's header, 0 included, and is not in the encrypted body); ITs `NotificationDispatcherIT.T_11_10_COMM_10_aReminderPushNeverOutlivesItsClassAndASeatOfferLivesUntilItsDeadline` (N-13 2 h ahead → TTL 7,200 s, 26 h ahead → 86,400 s; a retry after the start → `SKIPPED_STALE` «Push deadline passed», no call; club TTL 30 min → 1,800 s; N-15 FIFO 36 h ahead → 129,600 s; ALL_AT_ONCE and N-24 → 1,800 s; a lapsed offer → not pushed) and `RetentionSweepIT.T_14_20_COMM_10_theSweepPurgesPushSubscriptionsExpiredForNinetyDays` (expired 200 and 90 days ago → deleted, 89 days, `ACTIVE` and another club's → kept; the dry run counts 2 and deletes nothing; a second run 0). Each fails without the change.
- Changed test support: `EngineFixtures.ReminderFacts` (the engine ITs' double of S08/S09 for `ReminderDue`) answers the push deadline as S08/S09 now do (the start of the active booking it was given); no existing assertion changed.
- Run and passing: unit `PushTtlTest`, `PushDeadlineFactsTest`, `WebPushSenderTest` (13), `BookingNotificationFactsSurvivorsTest` (8), `BookingNotificationFactsSurvivors3Test`, `PrivacyJobsTest` (7), `ArchitectureTest` (23), `ArchitectureRulesTest` (23), `ClockRuleTest` (5); ITs `NotificationDispatcherIT` (29), `RetentionSweepIT` (7), `NotificationEngineIT` (16), `ErasureIT` (23), `RemindersJobIT` (7), `WaitlistIT` (23), `PushSubscriptionsIT` (4), `TrainingIT` (33).
- Assumption: «never delivered after the class start» caps the club's TTL (the shorter of the two applies), and «lives until its confirm-by deadline» is that deadline exactly, even past the club's TTL; an ALL_AT_ONCE offer has no confirm-by, so it keeps the club's TTL (as every other code). A push whose deadline has passed is `SKIPPED_STALE` (the notice no longer applies on that channel, CATALEG_NOTIFICACIONS rule 7), not `FAILED`. The deadline is read live (a class moved later moves it too).
- Assumption: 90 days is a product constant in code (`PushSubscriptionRetention.DAYS`), counted from `expiredAt`, not a new catalogue parameter: the decision fixes the value, and like the push failure count (3) or the unsubscribe token (30 days) it is not a club choice. Only `EXPIRED` subscriptions with an `expiredAt` are purged; an `ACTIVE` one is never touched by the sweep (an erased account's go with R-14-15 as before).
- Not changed: lock screens still show the notice's full title and body (pass 1's option (c) was not decided).
- For the owner: the privacy policy and the processing register should say that a device's push subscription (endpoint and keys) is deleted 90 days after it stops working or the member signs out on it.
- SPEC-CHANGE: docs/specs/S11-comunicacions.md R-11-07 (the TTL per code, the skipped push past its deadline, the 90-day purge); §5 new row «subscripció `EXPIRED` → esborrada»; T-11-10 (the COMM-10 cases); Canvis.
- SPEC-CHANGE: docs/specs/S14-tauler-auditoria-exportacions-rgpd.md R-14-16 new (h) (item `PURGE_EXPIRED_PUSH_SUBSCRIPTION`, counter `expiredPushSubscriptionsPurged`, 90 days) and the PRIV-02 confirmation list; T-14-20; Canvis. No catalogue change (no parameter, event, notification, error or audit action added; `messaging.push.ttlMinutes` keeps its row); no OpenAPI change (no route or schema).
- OTHER-REPO: none required (the service worker already shows what arrives; the TTL is the push service's).
- Review correction (review of p2-api-12-messaging-jobs, 14 Nov 2026): commit `fix(messaging): review correction [COMM-10]` puts the Javadoc of `BookingNotificationFacts.stillNotified` back on that method. The new `pushDeadline` had been inserted under it, leaving two stacked doc comments. Comment only, no behaviour change, so no test. Otherwise accepted. The TTL per code, the `SKIPPED_STALE` past the deadline and the 90-day purge match the decision, and the ITs pass (`NotificationDispatcherIT` 29, `RetentionSweepIT` 7, `PushSubscriptionsIT` 4, `RemindersJobIT` 7, `WaitlistIT` 23, `TrainingIT` 33).

## api-10-migration-cutover

### MIG-08 — FIXED
- Verified, no code touched. `migration:welcome` accepts only `--club`, `--batch`, `--interval`, `--only-numbers` and `--dry-run` (`MigrationWelcomeCommand.java:41-42`); without `--dry-run` it claims and sends at once, with no confirmation, environment check or eligible count printed first (:64-87). Candidates are every ACTIVE imported member with an account (`MigrationWelcomeRecipients.java:17-21`); neither the selection nor `MigrationWelcomeDelivery.deliver` (:41-45) looks at `lastLoginAt` or a password. The claim and `welcomeSentAt` are permanent (`AccountRepository.java:45-50,59`) and the runbook says the command "deliberately offers no force or claim-reset switch" (`docs/MIGRATION_WELCOME.md:152-153`). Since 8 Nov public delivery bypasses the allow-list (`EmailConfiguration.java:53`). The unconfirmed send form is the one S18 prescribes (R-18-16 and the CLI row, `S18-migracio-playoff.md:94,115`), and the runbook says the command refuses `--confirm-production` (`MIGRATION_WELCOME.md:29-30`).
- To decide (an S18 CLI change, with the runbook and `MigrationWelcomeCommandTest`): (a) how a send is armed: preview by default plus an explicit `--send`; `--confirm-production` as in `migration:playoff`; or the eligible count printed and the club slug typed back on stdin, as `migration:playoff --reset` does (`PlayoffCommand.java:43-46`); (b) whether accounts with a recorded login (`lastLoginAt`) or a password already set are skipped or still get the MIGRATED notice; (c) after an accidental send, whether the planned campaign needs a way to send again (an audited reset of `welcomeSentAt`, for example limited to accounts never logged in) or the accidental send stands.
- NOT-CODE meanwhile: run only the `--dry-run` form on the server, and keep the send forms (`MIGRATION_WELCOME.md:63-73`) out of shell history and shared snippets until the campaign is authorized.

Pass 2 (owner decision of 13 Nov 2026): `migration:welcome` previews by default (who would receive it, counts); sending needs an explicit flag and, on the prod profile, a typed confirmation.
- `MigrationWelcomeCommand`: new flag `--send` (no value; refused together with `--dry-run`). Without it the command runs only the preview pass (`MigrationWelcomeDelivery.deliver(..., dryRun=true)`: no claim, token, notification, e-mail or sleep) and prints one `WOULD SEND #<member number or -> <first name>[ (staff)]` line per ELIGIBLE account, in send order (staff first), then the existing `DRY RUN {…}` counts; `--dry-run` is kept and means the same. With `--send` outside the prod profile it sends as before (`WELCOME {…}`, exit 1 on REVIEW_REQUIRED). With `--send` on the prod profile (`Environment.matchesProfiles("prod", "production")`, as `migration:playoff`) it first prints that same preview, then `Production: type the club slug to send N welcome e-mails:` and reads one line from stdin; only the exact `--club` slug starts the send, anything else (another text, an empty line, end of input, an unreadable stdin) throws `PRODUCTION_REQUIRES_CONFIRMATION` (CLI exit 1) before any claim or e-mail. The send pass re-checks every account as before. The loop moved into a private `deliver(...)` used by both passes; the constructor takes the `Environment` (and, package-private, the stdin line supplier for tests).
- Tests added: unit `MigrationWelcomeCommandTest.MIG_08_withoutSendItPreviewsWhoWouldReceiveItAndTheCountsAndSendsNothing` (exact output for staff, numbered and unnumbered members, an already-sent account counted but not listed; the same with `--dry-run`; no send, sleep or prompt; outside prod `--send` sends without preview or prompt) and `MIG_08_onTheProdProfileASendNeedsTheClubSlugTypedBack` (null, empty, wrong case, leading space and another slug all refused with `PRODUCTION_REQUIRES_CONFIRMATION` and nothing sent; the slug → exact preview, prompt and `WELCOME {SENT=2}`; a prod preview asks nothing); IT `MigrationWelcomeCommandIT` (new, real Mongo and the test profile's fake sender: the default run lists both fictional members and writes no claim, `welcomeSentAt`, token or notification and sends no e-mail; a prod `--send` with the wrong slug writes and sends nothing, with the slug sends both and a later preview shows `ALREADY_SENT=2`). Each fails without the change.
- Changed tests (they asserted the old default that sends): in `MigrationWelcomeCommandTest`, `T_18_09_staffFirstSeparateBatchAndRerunSkipsDoNotConsumeTheSendBudget`, `T_18_09_uncertainDeliveryReturnsANonzeroCommandOutcomeWithoutRetrying`, `T_18_09_alreadySentRecipientsDoNotCauseBatchWaits` and `T_18_09_defaultBatchStopsAtFiftyWhenTheOperatorInterruptsTheWait` now pass `--send`; `T_18_09_invalidOptionsFailBeforeAnyAccountAccess` also refuses `--send=true`, a repeated `--send=yes` and `--send` with `--dry-run`; the constructors take the environment and the line supplier. No assertion was removed.
- Run and passing: unit `MigrationWelcomeCommandTest` (8), `CommandSchedulingGuardTest` (2), `ArchitectureTest` (23), `ArchitectureRulesTest` (23), `ErrorCatalogContractTest` (7); ITs `MigrationWelcomeCommandIT` (2), `MigrationWelcomeIT` (4).
- Assumption: «who would receive it» is the member number, the first name and whether the account is staff, in send order — never an e-mail address or a link (the existing rule for the operator's output). The list goes to the operator's terminal only; the runbook asks to keep it out of release evidence. The send's own report stays aggregate.
- Assumption: the typed confirmation is the club slug typed back on stdin, exactly, as `migration:playoff --reset` asks it (once, after a prompt that shows how many e-mails will go); there is no `--confirm-production` flag. «Prod profile» is the active Spring profile `prod` (or `production`), as `migration:playoff` reads it. Outside prod, `--send` alone sends.
- Not changed (pass 1's points (b) and (c) were not decided): accounts that already signed in still get the MIGRATED notice; `welcomeSentAt` and the claim stay permanent, with no reset.
- SPEC-CHANGE: docs/specs/S18-migracio-playoff.md R-18-16 (preview by default, `--send`, the typed slug on prod, `PRODUCTION_REQUIRES_CONFIRMATION`); §6 CLI row of `migration:welcome` (`[--dry-run | --send]`, its output); T-18-09 (the MIG-08 cases); Canvis. docs/MIGRATION_WELCOME.md (the send forms gain `--send`; the prod prompt; the preview's output; the Compose run without `-T` for a send on prod; the option table; the tests). No catalogue change (`PRODUCTION_REQUIRES_CONFIRMATION` is S18's CLI code); no route, so no OpenAPI change.

- Verified, no code touched. With `core.storage.mode=filesystem` (`MigrationReportConfiguration.java:22-25`) the named `report.xlsx` is written to the private volume with mode `rw-------` and no encryption (`VolumeMigrationReportStore.java:33-46`), while R-18-15 asks for an encrypted S3 object (`S18-migracio-playoff.md:93`). Downloads are a 5-minute signed link for the club ADMIN or platform admin, audited (`MigrationReports.java:56-65`); retention is fixed at 30 days (:24-27).

Pass 2 (owner decision of 13 Nov 2026): «once migrated it can be deleted directly» — an explicit way to purge a migration run's report right after the run instead of waiting for the 30-day retention: a `bin/core` command that removes the file and sets `reportPurgedAt`; idempotent.
- New command `migration:report-purge --club=<slug> --run=<id>` (`MigrationReportPurgeCommand`, a `CoreCommand` found by `CoreCli` like the others; scheduling stays off in a command process): inside the club's tenant it calls the new `MigrationReports.purgeNow(id)`, which, as P9's `purge`, deletes the object first through the configured `MigrationReportStore` (the volume's `Files.deleteIfExists`, or S3 with its unversioned-bucket guard) and only then records the purge with the new `MigrationRunRepository.reportPurgedNow` (tenant-scoped; `env = PRODUCTION` and `reportPurgedAt` still null → unset `reportFileKey`, `reportPurgedAt` = now, before or after the deadline). It prints `Migration report purged for run <id>`, or `Migration report of run <id> was already purged` on a repeat (which deletes the object again, covering a late upload, and changes nothing else). A storage failure throws before the mark (exit 1, retryable); an unknown run, another club's or a non-production one is `NOT_FOUND`. The existing guards then hold: the download route answers `EXPORT_EXPIRED`, `migration:report` cannot publish it again, P9 no longer lists it. The existing route is unchanged.
- Tests added: unit `MigrationReportsTest.MIG_13_theOperatorPurgesAReportBeforeItsDeadlineObjectFirstAndIdempotently` (delete then mark; a storage failure leaves it unmarked; an already purged run deletes again and answers false) and `MIG_13_onlyAKnownProductionRunOfTheClubCanBePurged`; `MigrationReportPurgeCommandTest` (2: tenant, both outputs; invalid options purge nothing); IT `MigrationReportsIT.MIG_13_theOperatorPurgesTheReportRightAfterTheRunIdempotently` (the command twice on a run 30 days before its deadline: object deleted twice, `reportPurgedAt` = the first run's instant, no pointer, run and manifest kept, no republication, P9 no longer lists it, the link 422 `EXPORT_EXPIRED` with no audit entry, another club's tenant `NOT_FOUND`). Each fails without the change.
- Run and passing: unit `MigrationReportsTest` (12), `MigrationReportPurgeCommandTest` (2), `MigrationReportCommandTest` (2), `CommandSchedulingGuardTest`, `ArchitectureTest`, `ArchitectureRulesTest`; ITs `MigrationReportsIT` (5), `PlayoffMigrationIT` (53, the production apply and report publication), `CleanupJobIT` (9, P9's purge).
- Assumption: a CLI command only (the decision's «and/or the existing route» leaves the route unchanged: no new HTTP surface for deleting personal data). It writes no audit entry, as P9's purge writes none; the operator's shell is the record. No confirmation prompt: the owner asked for a direct deletion, and the command names one run of one club.
- Not changed (not decided): in filesystem mode the report is still an unencrypted `rw-------` file on the private volume between its publication and the purge (pass 1's options (a) S3 with server-side encryption and (b) encryption in the app); the purge only shortens that window.
- For the owner: the purge does not reach copies of the file already in volume or database backups; they keep the backups' own retention (docs/DEPLOY.md, docs/INTERNAL_VOLUME_STORAGE.md).
- SPEC-CHANGE: docs/specs/S18-migracio-playoff.md R-18-15 (the operator's purge right after the run, its order, idempotency and answers); §6 new CLI row `migration:report-purge`; §11 test list (the MIG-13 case before T-18-16); Canvis. docs/MIGRATION_REPORTS.md (new «Purge a report right after the run» section), docs/INTERNAL_VOLUME_STORAGE.md (one sentence). No catalogue change (no error code, event, notification, parameter or audit action added); no route, so no OpenAPI change.
- Review note: reviewed 6b71090 against the decision (a `bin/core` command; the object is deleted first, then `reportPurgedAt`, tenant-scoped, production runs only; idempotent; the download, republication and P9 guards then refuse it; spec copies and runbooks). It implements the decision. Build green. Run again and passing: unit `MigrationReportsTest` (12), `MigrationReportPurgeCommandTest` (2), `MigrationReportCommandTest`, `CommandSchedulingGuardTest`, `ArchitectureTest`, `ArchitectureRulesTest`; ITs `MigrationReportsIT` (5), `CleanupJobIT` (9). For the owner: (1) the file is gone from the volume (or S3) but not from volume or database backups taken before the purge, which keep it for their own retention. (2) Run the purge only after `migration:playoff` or `migration:report` has finished. If it runs while a publication is uploading, the upload can land after the deletion; the publication then fails with `EXPORT_EXPIRED` but leaves the file on the volume. Running the purge again removes it (it always deletes the object again). (3) The purge writes no audit entry; the operator's shell is the only record.

## api-11-bookings-scheduling

### OUT-01 — FIXED
- `OutboxDispatcher.dispatch` (`shared/application/OutboxDispatcher.java`) runs each matching consumer in its own try: a consumer that throws no longer keeps the consumers after it from their delivery, on that attempt or on any retry. The record backs off (or ends FAILED) once every consumer has had its turn, storing the first failure's type; a retry still runs only the consumers without a `processedAt` checkpoint. No handler declares an order (no `@Order` on any `DomainEventHandler`; the map follows bean registration), so none relied on an earlier consumer having run. `docs/playbooks/new-event-consumer.md` says so.
- Tests: new `OutboxDispatcherTest` (3 cases, mocked repository) ran and passed. New `OutboxIT.OUT_01_aFailingConsumerDoesNotKeepTheConsumersAfterItFromTheirDelivery`: not run (MongoDB).
- Left, to decide: (a) how an operator lists and re-queues FAILED records (a `bin/core outbox:*` command, a platform-console screen, or both; who may; whether a re-queue resets `attempts` and is audited, a new S14 audit action); (b) an alert when `outbox.failed` > 0 or `outbox.pending` stays up (a new closed-catalogue notification like N-42, or an external check on the metric); (c) the retry horizon, today 10 attempts with back-off capped at 300 s, about 8.5 minutes (`SharedConfiguration.java:50`, `OutboxRepository.java:69`): hours ride out a provider outage but delay a poison event's FAILED state.
- NOT-CODE meanwhile: set `SENTRY_DSN`, and scrape or check `outbox_failed` on the management port's `/actuator/prometheus` (8081) daily during the first weeks.

Pass 2 (owner decision of 13 Nov 2026): the outbox retry horizon grows from about 8.5 minutes to about 24 hours (growing back-off), one failing consumer no longer starves the consumers after it, and when an event ends FAILED the platform admins are alerted.
- Starving: already done in pass 1 (each consumer in its own attempt, `OutboxDispatcher.dispatch`); unchanged and still covered by `OutboxDispatcherTest` and `OutboxIT.OUT_01_aFailingConsumerDoesNotKeepTheConsumersAfterItFromTheirDelivery` (both run here, passing).
- Horizon: `OutboxRepository.backoff(n)` = 2^(n−1) s up to `MAX_BACKOFF` (1 h; before, 300 s) after the n-th failed attempt, and `OutboxDispatcher.MAX_ATTEMPTS` = 36 (before, 10, in `SharedConfiguration`): 1, 2, 4 … 2,048 s, then hourly; the 36th attempt is about 24.1 h after the first. `OutboxRepository.failed` now answers whether that failure was the last one (the record is `FAILED`).
- Alert: new port `shared.application.OutboxFailureAlerts` (`failed(FailedEvent{id, clubId, type, aggregateType, attempts, error, failedAt})`, never the payload). When a record ends `FAILED` (its last attempt failed, or a claim beyond the attempts), the dispatcher logs one ERROR line `Outbox event FAILED after its retries` and calls every alert once, outside any transaction; an alert that throws is logged and changes nothing else. The application's alert is `identity.application.OutboxFailureNotices`: N-57 «Esdeveniment intern no processat» to every `ACTIVE` account with `AGILITYHUB_ADMIN` (as N-43), a SYSTEM e-mail through `SystemNotificationService.sendOnce` (product copy in the account's language, no club tenant), variables `club_name` (the club's name; «AgilityHub» for a record without a club), `reason` (the persisted error type) and `event_type`; each recipient's row id derives from code, event type, UTC day and account, so an admin gets at most one per event type and day. It is a direct call, not another outbox event (an alert about the outbox does not wait in the outbox, and nothing can loop). New catalogue entry N-57 (`NotificationCatalog`, SYSTEM, ADMINS → EMAIL, no event, like N-53), its texts in `messages_{ca,es,en}`, `SystemEmailRenderer.CODES`.
- Tests added: unit `OutboxDispatcherTest.OUT_01_aRecordThatEndsFailedTellsEveryAlertOnceAndAnEarlierFailureNone` (the last failure tells both alerts, the first of which throws, with the record's fields; a failure that leaves it PENDING tells nobody) and `OUT_01_theRetryHorizonIsAboutADayWithAGrowingBackOff` (the waits, the cap and a 23–25 h horizon over the 36 attempts); ITs `OutboxIT.OUT_01_aRecordThatRunsOutOfItsAttemptsIsFailedAndAlertedOnce` (a consumer bug on one club's event: PENDING after 2 attempts, FAILED at the 3rd, one alert with ids, type, attempts and error type even though the first alert throws; another club's event published; nothing more two days later) and `identity.application.OutboxFailureNoticesIT.OUT_01_anEventThatRunsOutOfItsRetriesEmailsEveryActivePlatformAdminOncePerTypeAndDay` (through a dispatcher with the real `OutboxFailureNotices`: one e-mail to each active platform admin, none to a blocked one or a plain account, `clubId` null, the Catalan and English subjects, the type, club name and error type in the text and nothing of the payload; the same type the same day → no second e-mail; another type (a club-less record: «AgilityHub») or the next day → one more each). Each fails without the change.
- Changed tests (they asserted the old behaviour or counted the catalogue): `OutboxIT.E0_T04_maxAttemptsFailsAndBackoffCapsAtFiveMinutes` → `E0_T04_OUT_01_maxAttemptsFailsAndBackoffGrowsToAnHour` (14 attempts, `min(3600, 2^(n−1))`; its FAILED and «not claimed again» assertions unchanged); N-57 counted in `NotificationSpecTest` (64 codes, ends with N-57), `NotificationCatalogContractTest` (Annex A 21 rows; N-50, N-53 and N-57 have no event), `MessageTemplateSeedTest` (N-57 without a template) and `NotificationMatrixTest.WITHOUT_EVENT`; the `NotificationEngineIT` comment on the attempts.
- Run and passing: unit `OutboxDispatcherTest` (5), `NotificationCatalogContractTest` (4), `NotificationSpecTest` (6), `NotificationMatrixTest` (2,777), `MessageTemplateSeedTest` (5), `MessageTemplateSeedChecksTest` (3), `NotificationSeedSnapshotTest` (3), `TemplateClubSeedTest` (5), `SystemEmailBrandingTest` (4), `MessageParityTest`, `EventCatalogContractTest` (3), `NotificationLinksTest` (41), `ChannelResolverTest` (69), `E3`/`E4`/`E5`/`E6`/`E8`/`E9ResponseContractTest`, `ArchitectureTest`, `ArchitectureRulesTest`, `ClockRuleTest`, `SeedAndWriterRulesTest`; ITs `OutboxIT` (14), `OutboxFailureNoticesIT` (1), `DomainRecheckIT` (6, N-43's path), `SignupFollowUpFixesIT` (15) and `FollowupIT` (29) (consumers retried through the outbox), `NotificationEngineIT` (16).
- Assumption: «a growing back-off over about 24 hours» keeps the existing doubling from 1 s (fast retries for transient conflicts) and caps it at one hour, so the last attempts are hourly and the event ends FAILED within an hour of the 24-hour mark; one product constant (36 attempts), no new parameter (they are E0 infrastructure settings, `docs/playbooks/new-event-consumer.md`).
- Assumption: «the platform admins» are the active accounts with `AGILITYHUB_ADMIN`, by e-mail (as N-43, the one platform-admin notice of the catalogue; the console has no notification feed). «Alerted» is at most one e-mail per admin, event type and UTC day: a consumer bug fails every event of its type, and one e-mail per event would spend the e-mail provider's daily allowance that members' sign-in links share (COMM-07). The other failures of that type stay counted by `outbox.failed` and logged one ERROR line each. A failed send of N-57 is not retried (the SYSTEM e-mail path sends once, as N-43).
- Not changed: there is still no tool to list or re-queue FAILED records (pass 1's option (a) was not decided); the e-mail tells the operator where to look (`domain_events`, `status: FAILED`).
- SPEC-CHANGE: docs/specs/00-transversal/CATALEG_ESDEVENIMENTS.md rule 2 (each consumer its own attempt; the growing back-off over about 24 hours, 36 attempts; FAILED → N-57 to the platform admins, at most one per admin, type and UTC day). No event added.
- SPEC-CHANGE: docs/specs/00-transversal/CATALEG_NOTIFICACIONS.md new Annex A row N-57 «Esdeveniment intern no processat» (SYSTEM, admins de plataforma → EMAIL, `club_name`, `reason`, S17) and `event_type` (N-57) in the Annex's «Variables noves».
- SPEC-CHANGE: docs/specs/S17-consola-de-clubs.md §8 (N-57 row), Canvis; docs/playbooks/new-event-consumer.md (back-off and attempts); docs/DEPLOY.md telemetry paragraph; seeds/README.md (N-57 has no template). No OpenAPI change (no route or schema).
- OTHER-REPO: none required (an e-mail; no screen shows outbox records).
- Review note (review of p2-api-12-messaging-jobs, 14 Nov 2026): accepted; the ITs pass (`OutboxIT` 14, `OutboxFailureNoticesIT` 1, `NotificationEngineIT` 16). The owner should know five things. (1) N-57 is capped at one e-mail per admin, event type and UTC day. Further events of the same type that fail on the same day only add an ERROR log line and raise `outbox.failed`. (2) The alert goes only by SYSTEM e-mail through the platform sender. If e-mail delivery is disabled, the row is stored `SKIPPED_NOT_ALLOWED` and nobody is told. Nobody is told either if no `ACTIVE` `AGILITYHUB_ADMIN` account has a monitored mailbox. Manual step before launch: check both. (3) `reason` is in practice always `IllegalStateException`, because the dispatcher wraps every consumer failure. So the e-mail names the event type and club but not the cause, which is in the logs and Sentry. (4) An event now keeps being retried for up to about 24 h. A consumer that recovers late applies the event late, and possibly after later events of the same aggregate: the outbox has no per-aggregate ordering. This was already possible within the old 8.5 minutes, and the window is now a day. (5) There is still no tool to list or re-queue `FAILED` records.

### JOB-01 — FIXED
- Verified: a run with item errors ends PARTIAL (`JobRunner.java:234` at `audit-base`), `complete()` publishes `JobFailed` (N-42) only for FAILED (:295), and `occurrenceTaken` (`JobRunRepository.java:46-49`) counts a FAILED or PARTIAL scheduled row, so a daily process waits for the next day or [Executa ara]. That is what S15 prescribes (R-15-10, and §5 "Un FAILED/PARTIAL no es reintenta sol", `S15-processos-programats.md:97,233`). The net S15 names for PARTIAL, Sentry (R-15-07 "Sentry rep FAILED/PARTIAL"), was missing: only `ApiExceptionHandler` captured events, so setting `SENTRY_DSN` alone would not have reported any job.
- Changed: `JobRunner.report` sends one Sentry event per FAILED or PARTIAL run that is not a dry run, from the call that closed it (holder or reaper), with the first exception, if any, and the first error's trace id (the one the job's error log line prints); nothing is sent without `SENTRY_DSN`. `docs/DEPLOY.md` (telemetry) says so.
- Tests: new `JobRunSentryReportTest` (3 cases) ran and passed, as did `ArchitectureTest` and `ArchitectureRulesTest`. New `JobFrameworkIT.T_15_10_failedAndPartialRunsAreReportedToSentryOnce`: not run (MongoDB).
- To decide (R-15-10 and §5 changes): (a) whether `JobFailed` → N-42 also fires for PARTIAL, for every process or only those with member-facing effects (P2 risk review, P3 no-show notices, P4 reminders, P5 expirations), and from which error count; (b) whether a FAILED or PARTIAL daily occurrence is retried the same day (for example one CATCH_UP retry after N minutes, which R-15-04's per-item marks make safe) or keeps waiting for the next day or [Executa ara].
- NOT-CODE: set `SENTRY_DSN` before L4 with an alert rule on new issues; during the first weeks an admin checks D11 (run history) daily.

Pass 2 (owner decision of 13 Nov 2026): when a scheduled job that affects members (risk review, reminders, expiries, billing-related runs) ends PARTIAL or FAILED, the club's admins are alerted (a notification of the closed catalogue), once per run.
- `JobCatalog.alertsOnPartial(JobName)`: the processes that affect members are `RISK_REVIEW` (P2), `REMINDERS` (P4), `EXPIRATIONS` (P5) and the billing-related `PAYMENT_TIMEOUTS` (P7) and `BILLING_REMINDER` (P10). `JobRunner.complete` publishes `JobFailed` when `JobRunner.alerts(run)` holds: a FAILED run of any process, as before, or a PARTIAL run of one of those five — in the same transaction as the run's `SchedulerRun`, only from the call that closed the run, never for a dry run, one event per run whatever its number of failed items. `JobFailed{job, runId, status: PARTIAL, errorCount}` takes the existing path to N-42 «Procés automàtic amb errors» (ADMINS, APP + EMAIL, `job_name`, `error_count`) under `jobs.alertAdminsOnFailure`; `Job#failed` still runs for FAILED runs only. No new notification, event or parameter.
- Tests added: unit `JobPartialAlertsTest.T_15_10_JOB_01_aPartialRunAlertsOnlyForTheProcessesThatAffectMembers` (every `JobName` × FAILED, PARTIAL, SUCCEEDED, SKIPPED, RUNNING); IT `JobFrameworkIT.T_15_10_JOB_01_aPartialRunOfAProcessThatAffectsMembersAlertsTheAdminsOnce` (a PARTIAL risk-review run → one `JobFailed{status: PARTIAL, errorCount: 1}` → N-42 APP + EMAIL to the club's admin; the same event redelivered → nothing more; a second PARTIAL run the same local day → its own event, no second N-42; a PARTIAL clean-up run → no `JobFailed`), and `E8LeaveProcessesIT.T_15_21_anS09FailureRollsBackTheWholeP5ItemAndReportsPartial` now also asserts that the real P5 run's PARTIAL publishes one `JobFailed{job: EXPIRATIONS, status: PARTIAL}` (an added assertion; its earlier ones are unchanged). Each fails without the change. `JobFrameworkIT.T_15_10_itemFailuresArePartialJobFailuresAlertAdminsOncePerLocalDay` still asserts that a PARTIAL run of the framework's test process (not member-facing) publishes no `JobFailed`: unchanged.
- Run and passing: unit `JobPartialAlertsTest`, `JobRunSentryReportTest` (3), `JobCatalogContractTest` (5), `ArchitectureTest`, `EventCatalogContractTest` (3), `NotificationCatalogContractTest` (4), `NotificationSpecTest` (6), `E5ResponseContractTest` (4); ITs `JobFrameworkIT` (18), `RiskReviewJobIT` (9), `E8ScheduledProcessesIT` (17), `E8LeaveProcessesIT` (2), `JobsApiIT` (8), `DomainRecheckIT` (6), `RemindersJobIT` (7).
- Assumption: «jobs that affect members (risk review, reminders, expiries, billing-related runs)» is that list read as given: P2, P4, P5 and, as billing-related runs, P7 (payment timeouts: a member's unpaid booking or checkout) and P10 (the monthly remittance reminder). P1 week opening, P3 no-show notices, P6 waitlist FIFO, P8 class finishing, P9 clean-up, `erasures` and `retention-sweep` are not in it: their PARTIAL runs still reach only Sentry and D11 (one entry in `JobCatalog` adds a process).
- Assumption: «once per run» is one alert per run, never one per failed item (one `JobFailed` per closed run), and N-42 keeps R-15-10's cap of one per process and club-local day (its occurrence key `N-42:{job}:{day}`) and its club switch `jobs.alertAdminsOnFailure` (default on): a continuous process such as the reminders, which runs every minute, would otherwise send an e-mail a minute while it keeps failing an item. A FAILED run was already alerted (unchanged). The «notification in the closed catalogue» is the existing N-42.
- Not changed: a FAILED or PARTIAL daily occurrence is still not retried the same day (pass 1's option (b) was not decided).
- SPEC-CHANGE: docs/specs/S15-processos-programats.md R-15-10 (N-42 also for a PARTIAL run of P2, P4, P5, P7, P10: one `JobFailed{status: PARTIAL}` per run, same switch and daily cap); §5 transition RUNNING → PARTIAL; §8 N-42 row; T-15-10 (the JOB-01 cases); Canvis.
- SPEC-CHANGE: docs/specs/00-transversal/CATALEG_NOTIFICACIONS.md N-42 row (its trigger: FAILED, a missed occurrence, or PARTIAL of a process that affects members); docs/specs/00-transversal/CATALEG_ESDEVENIMENTS.md `JobFailed` (its `status` values). No new catalogue entry.
- SPEC-CHANGE: docs/DEPLOY.md telemetry paragraph (N-42 besides Sentry). No OpenAPI change.
- OTHER-REPO: none required (D11 and the notification feed already show N-42 and the run's PARTIAL status).
- Review note (review of p2-api-12-messaging-jobs, 14 Nov 2026): accepted; the ITs pass (`JobFrameworkIT` 18, `E8LeaveProcessesIT` 2, `RiskReviewJobIT` 9, `E8ScheduledProcessesIT` 17, `RemindersJobIT` 7). Two business readings for the owner. (1) «Once per run» is implemented as one `JobFailed` per run, but N-42 keeps its cap of one per process and club-local day. A second PARTIAL run of the same process that day sends no N-42. PARTIAL and FAILED share that daily slot (`N-42:{job}:{day}` in `CommonNotificationFacts`), so an early PARTIAL now suppresses the N-42 of a FAILED run of the same process later that day; before JOB-01 that FAILED would have been alerted. Example: a reminders run that misses one booking at 08:00, then a reminders outage at 15:00, gives one e-mail, not two. The FAILED run still reaches Sentry and D11. If the owner wants every run alerted, or FAILED kept separate from PARTIAL, the occurrence key needs the run id or the status. (2) PARTIAL alerts cover P2, P4, P5, P7 and P10 only. A PARTIAL run of P3 (no-show notices), P6 (waitlist FIFO) or P1 (week opening), which also reach members, still alerts no one.

### BOOK-01 — NEEDS-DECISION
- Verified, no code touched. `POST /bookings/{id}/cancellation` admits a plain INSTRUCTOR token (`BookingsController.java:213-226`), which sees every booking of the club (`BookingQueryService.java:38-42`, `staff=true`) and cancels with `INSTRUCTOR_NOTICE` while `bookings.instructorLastMinuteNotice` is true, its default (`BookingCancellationService.java:45-54`); nothing checks that the caller teaches the class or that the class is today, unlike the attendance sheet (`AttendanceSheetService.java:52-54`). That is what S08 prescribes: the endpoint table gives the route to "INSTRUCTOR (si paràmetre)" (`S08-reserves-de-classes-i-llista-espera.md:204`) and R-08-10 lists the instructor among who may cancel (:110). The web never calls the route with a plain instructor token: «ha avisat» is the sheet's `PUT` (S10 R-10-05), and screen 22's «Gestiona les reserves» uses an `INSTRUCTOR_BOOKINGS` impersonation token, which takes the MEMBER branch (`ImpersonationService.java:64-70`).
- To decide (S08 R-08-10 and endpoint table, the route's OpenAPI description, T-08-18): (a) drop the plain-instructor branch so that only the sheet records «ha avisat»; (b) keep it but require the caller to be one of the class's instructors (as the sheet does) and possibly the class date to be today, and say whether that cancellation gets an audit entry (a new S14 action in the closed catalogue); (c) keep it as is.

### PLAN-01 — NEEDS-DECISION
- Verified, no code touched. `club.holidays` is read only by week generation, which skips template classes on a holiday (`WeekGenerationUseCase.java:100-108`), and by the training grid (`TrainingContext.java:59`, `TrainingBookingService.java:76-80`); a change only invalidates the grid cache (`TrainingConsumers.java:25,32-34`). Nothing cancels or flags ACTIVE classes or ACTIVE training bookings already on a date added later. S02 R-02-09 names only those consumers (`S02-club-configuracio-i-parametres.md:70`).
- To decide (S02 R-02-09, S06, S09; possibly a new error or notification in the closed catalogues): (a) `PUT /club/holidays` that adds a date with ACTIVE classes or live training bookings answers with the affected list and needs an explicit confirmation, then cancels them through `ClassCancellationUseCase` and the training `cancelByClub` (which reason, and which text the members receive); (b) only list the affected classes and bookings in D11 for the admin to cancel by hand; (c) leave it as a documented manual step.
- NOT-CODE meanwhile: enter the season's holidays before generating weeks; for a closure added later, cancel that day's classes from D4, then block each ring for the day with `cancelBookings: true` (R-06-11), which cancels the training bookings with a notice.

### BOOK-02 — FIXED
- Verified, no code touched. A class booking is refused only for a second live booking of the dog in the same class (`BookingChecks.java:56-58`, `ALREADY_BOOKED`); nothing compares its time with the dog's other class bookings or its training bookings. The training path checks both directions (`TrainingBookingService.java:86-89`, S09 R-09-10 step 3, `422 DOG_ALREADY_BOOKED`). S08 has no such rule.
- To decide (a new S08 rule): whether a dog may hold overlapping class bookings, or a class booking over its own training slot; if not, which code answers (`DOG_ALREADY_BOOKED` is S09's in `CATALEG_ERRORS.md:34`), whether PAYMENT_PENDING bookings count, whether the swap (R-08-08) and the waiting-list claim (R-08-15) apply the same check, and whether a class booking over the dog's training booking is refused or cancels the training.

Pass 2 (owner decision of 13 Nov 2026): a dog cannot hold two class bookings that overlap in time, nor a class booking over its own free-training slot; the second booking is refused with a clear error (DOG_ALREADY_BOOKED reused, its meaning fits); PAYMENT_PENDING bookings count; the same rule applies to the swap and to the waiting-list claim.
- New `BookingChecks.noOverlap(subject, swappedBookingId)` (S08 pipeline): the dog's live class bookings (`ACTIVE`, `PAYMENT_PENDING`: the existing `BookingRepository.liveOverlapping`, `classStartsAt < endsAt` and `classEndsAt > startsAt`, so back-to-back classes do not overlap) of another class, and its `ACTIVE` training bookings over the class, through a new port `bookings.application.ports.DogTrainingOverlapPort` with its adapter `training.application.DogTrainingOverlap` (the same `TrainingBookingRepository.activeForDog` query as R-09-10 step 3; `clubs.bookings` still never imports `clubs.training`). Either → `422 DOG_ALREADY_BOOKED` (S09's code, «El gos ja té una reserva en aquest horari»; no new code). `BookingConfirmationService.confirmInTransaction` calls it right after `notBookedYet`, inside the transaction and after the owner lock (`lockOwner`, which the training booking takes too, so a concurrent class confirmation and training booking of one owner serialise). That one transaction is the confirmation (`POST /bookings`), the swap (`swapBookingId`: the booking it cancels does not count) and the claim (`POST /waitlist-entries/{id}/claim` → `WaitlistService.claim` → the same confirmation). Both routes list `DOG_ALREADY_BOOKED` in `@ContractErrors` and say so in their OpenAPI description; roles, fixtures, idempotency and audit are unchanged (a refused confirmation writes nothing, its 422 is stored for its key as any other).
- Tests added: unit `BookingChecksOverlapTest` (3: an ACTIVE and a PAYMENT_PENDING booking of another class at that time refuse; an ACTIVE training booking refuses; nothing at that time, or only the swapped booking, lets it through and another booking id does not); IT `BookingsIT.BOOK_02_aDogIsNeverBookedIntoTwoClassesAtTheSameTimeNorOverItsOwnTraining` (Duna in Thursday 18:50–19:50: the 19:20 class → 422 and no booking, counter or event; 19:50 back to back and another dog at 19:20 → 201; Rock with an ACTIVE training booking 19:00–19:30 → the 18:50 class 422; a CANCELLED training booking does not count), `…_aPaymentPendingBookingAlsoHoldsTheDogsTime` (PAY_TO_BOOK), `…_theSwapMovesTheDogToAParallelClassButNeverOverAnotherOfItsBookings` (at the W1 limit of 2: a swap whose new class overlaps the dog's other booking → 422, bookings unchanged; a swap to the class parallel to the swapped booking → 201, `CANCELLED{SWAP}`), `…_theWaitingListClaimIsRefusedWhileTheDogIsBookedAtThatTime` (FIFO offer: the claim → 422, the entry stays `NOTIFIED`, no booking; after cancelling the overlapping booking the same hold claims → 201, `CONSOLIDATED`). With the call to `noOverlap` removed the four ITs fail (checked).
- Changed test: `BookingChecksSurvivors3Test` passes the new port to the `BookingChecks` constructor (no assertion changed).
- Run and passing: unit `BookingChecksOverlapTest` (3), `BookingChecksSurvivors3Test`, `BookingConfirmationServiceTest`, `BookingConfirmationServiceSurvivorsTest`, `BookingConfirmationServiceSurvivors3Test`, `SeatHoldServiceSurvivorsTest`, `WaitlistServiceSurvivorsTest`/`2`/`3`, `BookingPortsTest`, `ArchitectureTest` (23), `ArchitectureRulesTest` (23), `ErrorCatalogContractTest` (7), `S08MemberFlowContractTest` (8), `E5RouteParityTest`; ITs 40 classes, every one that books a class, in six Maven runs on a copy of this tree: `OpenApiSnapshotTest` (12, after `bin/openapi-snapshot`), `SecurityInventoryIT` (4), `BookingsIT` (30), `WaitlistIT` (23), `BookingConcurrencyIT` (8), `MemberFlowContractIT` (6), `AttendanceIT` (22), `BackOfficeContractIT` (8), `BookingJobsIT` (3), `BookingLanesOffIT` (4), `ClassFinishingJobIT` (6), `CleanupJobIT` (9), `E8LeaveProcessesIT` (2), `InstructorAggregatesIT` (10), `JobTriggerRetryIT` (4), `JobsApiIT` (8), `LifecycleConcurrencyIT` (7), `LifecycleCurlIT` (1), `LifecycleIT` (46), `LifecycleMenuIT` (5), `MemberAggregatesIT` (7), `NoShowNoticesJobIT` (5), `NotificationActionsIT` (15), `RemindersJobIT` (7), `RiskReviewJobIT` (9), `SingleClassCheckoutIT` (12), `WeekOpeningJobIT` (4), `TrainingIT` (33), `DemoAttendanceSeedIT` (3), `DemoBillingSeedIT` (2), `DemoPlanningSeedIT` (10), `DemoScenarioSeedIT` (6), `DemoSeedRetryIT` (1), `DemoSeedsIT` (12), `DashboardIT` (11), `E5ContractIT` (41), `ListFieldsContractIT` (4), `ResponseNullabilityIT` (1), `DemoCoursesSeedIT` (1), `DemoPlatformAccountsSeedIT` (2): 0 failures, 0 errors (the demo seeds book no dog twice at one time).
- Assumption: the check belongs to the booking itself (the confirmation, and so the swap and the claim, as the decision lists); `POST /seat-holds` and joining a waiting list (`POST /waitlist-entries`) are not checked. A member can therefore hold a seat or wait for a class at the time of another booking of the dog, and cancel that booking before confirming or claiming (the claim IT shows it); a hold refused at confirmation is released when the member leaves the page, as for any other refusal.
- Assumption: in a swap, the booking being swapped does not count (it is cancelled in the same transaction), so a dog may move to a class at the same time as the one it leaves; any other overlapping booking refuses the swap. «Overlap» is open intervals on the class times stored on the booking (`classStartsAt`/`classEndsAt`): one class ending at 19:50 and another starting at 19:50 do not overlap. A training booking counts only while `ACTIVE` (as R-09-10 step 3), whatever the FREE_TRAINING module's state; class bookings `CANCELLED*` never count.
- Not changed (no decision asked for it): a class moved later in time (R-06-09 edit) is not re-checked against its bookings' other bookings; the bookable list (screen 04) still shows a class at the time of another booking as bookable.
- SPEC-CHANGE: docs/specs/S08-reserves-de-classes-i-llista-espera.md R-08-08 (the overlap rule, `422 DOG_ALREADY_BOOKED`, not at hold or join), R-08-09 (the swap; the swapped booking does not count), R-08-15 (the claim; the entry stays `NOTIFIED`); §6 rows `POST /bookings` and `POST /waitlist-entries/{id}/claim` (422 `DOG_ALREADY_BOOKED`); §6 own error codes (`DOG_ALREADY_BOOKED`, reused from S09); §11 test list (the BOOK-02 case before T-08-18); Canvis.

The `errors:DOG_ALREADY_BOOKED` texts (`packages/i18n/src/locales/*/errors.json`) should say «at this time», as the API's message does: «Aquest gos ja té una reserva a aquesta hora.» / «Este perro ya tiene una reserva a esta hora.» / «This dog already has a booking at this time.» (they are shared with S09's training booking, where the same sentence applies). A unit test of the confirmation page for that code (message shown, back to 04). The back-office «book for the member» flow (impersonation) uses the same routes and gets the same answer.
- Review note: reviewed 72d37ea against the decision (the check runs inside the confirmation transaction after the dog owner's lock, which the S09 training booking also takes on the dog's owner, so a concurrent class confirmation and training booking of the same dog are serialised; tenant-scoped queries; `PAYMENT_PENDING` counts; the swap and the claim go through the same code; catalogue row, spec copies and snapshot). It implements the decision. Build green. Run again and passing: unit `BookingChecksOverlapTest`, `BookingChecksSurvivors3Test`, `BookingConfirmationService*Test`, `WaitlistService*Test`, `SeatHoldServiceSurvivorsTest`, `BookingPortsTest`, `ErrorCatalogContractTest`, `S08MemberFlowContractTest`, `ArchitectureTest`, `ArchitectureRulesTest`; ITs `BookingsIT` (30), `WaitlistIT` (23), `BookingConcurrencyIT` (8), `TrainingIT` (33), `SingleClassCheckoutIT` (12), `MemberFlowContractIT` (6), `SecurityInventoryIT`, `E4ContractIT`, `E5ContractIT`, `OpenApiSnapshotTest` (the committed snapshot matches). The implementer's report of the four ITs failing without the call is consistent with a leftover surefire report of that run. For the owner: (1) the hold (`POST /seat-holds`) and joining a waiting list are not checked, as the log's assumption says. A member can wait for a class at the time of another of the dog's bookings and gets the refusal only when claiming the offer, so the offered seat stays held for that dog until the offer expires, unless the member cancels the other booking first. (2) Paths that bring an existing booking back to ACTIVE are not new bookings and are not checked: the instructor undoing a notice (`BookingCancellationService.restoreNotice`) and a class whose time is edited (R-06-09). Either can still leave a dog with two overlapping bookings in rare cases.
### PLAN-02 — FIXED
- Verified, no code touched. `ClassCancellationUseCase.cancel` checks only the transition ACTIVE → CANCELLED (`ClassCancellationUseCase.java:26-42`, `ClassSessionRules.java:9-16`), so a class that has started, or ended less than `classes.finishGraceMinutes` (15) ago, can still be cancelled: its bookings become CANCELLED_BY_CLUB, pack sessions are refunded and N-08a goes out. R-06-10 sets only `state ∈ {DRAFT, ACTIVE}` as precondition (`S06-planificacio-setmanes-i-calendari.md:123`). The risk review skips started classes (`RiskReviewJob.java:55,74`) and the SCH-01 restoration requires `startsAt > now`.
- To decide (an R-06-10 change and the D4c modal): (a) refuse once `startsAt ≤ now` with `409 INVALID_STATE`; (b) allow it after the start only with an explicit confirmation flag, and decide whether a class that took place refunds packs and keeps its attendance marks; (c) keep it, relying on the D4c modal.

Pass 2 (owner decision of 13 Nov 2026): cancelling a class is refused once its start time has passed (409 INVALID_STATE); the back-office modal no longer offers it.
- New `ClassSessionRules.cancellableByAdmin(startsAt, now)` (INVALID_STATE unless `startsAt > now`). `ClassCancellationUseCase` gains `cancelByAdmin(...)`, the same R-06-10 transaction with that check right after the state transition check, before `ADMIN_TEXT_REQUIRED` and before anything is read or written; `SchedulingController.cancelClass` (`POST /class-sessions/{id}/cancellation`, ADMIN, `Idempotency-Key`, unchanged roles, fixtures and errors: `INVALID_STATE` was already listed) now calls it, and its OpenAPI description says so. The other callers keep `cancel(...)` unchanged: S15's risk review (which already skips a started class, `skippedStarted`), S07's activity publication with `cancelClasses` (through `RingBlockService.resolve`) and the demo seeds.
- Tests added: unit `CalendarRulesTest.PLAN_02_theAdminCancelsOnlyAClassThatHasNotStarted` (one second ahead passes; at the start, a second after and a null start are INVALID_STATE); IT `CalendarIT.PLAN_02_theAdminCannotCancelOrDeleteAClassOnceItHasStarted` (an ACTIVE class with 2 bookings, 1 waiting entry and 2 holds: at its start, while it runs and 10 minutes after its end, still ACTIVE, both `CLUB_MANUAL` and `DELETED` with a text answer 409 INVALID_STATE, nothing changes — state, counters, bookings, waiting list, holds, no `ClassCancelledByClub` or `PackRefunded`; one second before the start it is cancelled; a draft of the following week cannot be deleted once its start has passed). Both fail without the change.
- Run and passing: unit `CalendarRulesTest`, `E5RouteParityTest`; ITs `CalendarIT` (27), `E4ContractIT` (65), `E5ContractIT` (41), `TrainingIT` (33), `DemoPlanningSeedIT` (10), `WaitlistIT` (23), `InstructorAggregatesIT` (10), `BookingsIT` (26) — every IT that calls the route — and `OpenApiSnapshotTest` (12) after `bin/openapi-snapshot`.
- Assumption: the rule covers both buttons of the route ([ANUL·LA LA CLASSE] `CLUB_MANUAL` and [ELIMINA] `DELETED`) and any state, so a DRAFT whose start has passed can no longer be deleted either (it never had bookings and stays invisible to members); «started» is `startsAt ≤ now` (the second of the start included), the same boundary as SCH-01's restoration.
- Assumption: the decision concerns the admin's cancellation of the class (D4c). S07's activity publication with `cancelClasses: true` still cancels a conflicting class that has already started (an activity whose ring block begins while the class runs on the same ring) — not changed here; it would need its own decision. S15's review already never cancels a started class.
- SPEC-CHANGE: docs/specs/S06-planificacio-setmanes-i-calendari.md R-06-10 «Precondicions» (the ADMIN's cancellation and deletion require `startsAt > ara`, else 409 `INVALID_STATE`, nothing changes; D4c no longer offers them for a started class); §5 transition table rows DRAFT → CANCELLED and ACTIVE → CANCELLED; §6 row `POST /class-sessions/{id}/cancellation` (409 `INVALID_STATE` also once started); §11 test list (the PLAN-02 case after T-06-14); Canvis. No catalogue change (no error code, event, notification, parameter or audit action added).

- OTHER-REPO: web clubs-admin `apps/clubs-admin/src/planning/SelectedClassCard.tsx`: show «Anul·la la classe» (`CLUB_MANUAL`) and «Elimina» / «Elimina l'esborrany» (`DELETED`) only while `Date.parse(session.startsAt) > Date.now()` (as `isRestorable` in `RestoreClassModal.tsx` does), so the D4c modal (`CancelClassModal`) is never opened for a class that has started; no new text (the buttons simply are not there, the card's other actions stay). A `409 INVALID_STATE` from `POST /class-sessions/{id}/cancellation` (a class that started while the modal was open) keeps today's error handling and refetch. Unit test: the card of a started ACTIVE class and of a past DRAFT has neither button; one a minute ahead has both. API route unchanged (ADMIN, body `{reason, adminText?}`, `Idempotency-Key`); only the new refusal.

It implements the decision. Build green. Run again and passing: unit `CalendarRulesTest`, `E5RouteParityTest`; ITs `CalendarIT` (27), `E4ContractIT` (65), `E5ContractIT` (41), `BookingsIT`, `WaitlistIT`, `OpenApiSnapshotTest` (the committed snapshot matches). For the owner: (1) the refusal is on the ADMIN's route only. Publishing or editing an S07 activity with `cancelClasses: true` (`RingBlockService.resolve`, ADMIN) still cancels a conflicting class that has already started, with its bookings, pack refunds and N-08a. (2) The same route also deletes drafts (`reason = DELETED`), so a DRAFT whose start time has passed can no longer be deleted; it stays a draft (no bookings, not visible to members) and would become ACTIVE if its week is validated later.
