## api-12-tests-release

### REL-02 — FIXED
- `GET /api/v1/health` (UP and DOWN) also returns `commit` (required, `string | null`): the git commit of the running build. CI passes `GIT_COMMIT=${{ github.sha }}` to the image build (`.github/workflows/ci.yml`); the `Dockerfile` hands it to Maven (`-Dgit.commit`) and labels the runtime image `org.opencontainers.image.revision`; `pom.xml` records it in build-info (property `git.commit`, default `unknown`, which the health answers as `null`). Like `version`, it is public. `docs/openapi/openapi.json` (edited by hand), `docs/openapi/CHANGELOG.md`, the README, `docs/DEPLOY.md` step 5 (check the commit after a deploy) and `bin/e1-smoke`'s DOWN envelope changed with it.
- Tests: new `HealthCommitTest` (2) and the adjusted `HealthControllerTest` (an envelope of 4, `commit` as recorded), plus `OpenApiConfigurationTest` and `DockerfileSeedsTest`: ran and passed. Not run: `OpenApiSnapshotTest` (IT), an image build, `deploy/test_e11_t09.py` `MavenRetryTest` (it downloads the wrapper's Maven; it now also passes the inert `-Dgit.commit` token) and `bin/e1-smoke`.
- NOT-CODE: tag both repositories at the deployed commits and record, per release, the image digest and the three SPA hashes that `bin/release-preflight` prints; name the rollback target of each release. Freeze feature deployments through the launch and the first remittance.
- To decide: real version numbers instead of `0.1.0-SNAPSHOT` / web `0.0.0`, and whether the changelogs get version headings; a stored data-version marker (and a check of the previous image on current data) so that "rollback is safe" can be stated per release; the `release` branch of `roadmap/README.md:31,54`: retire it, or have CI build and publish from it (today only `main` does, `.github/workflows/ci.yml:4-7,159,165,171`).
- OTHER-REPO: web — embed the commit in each bundle (for example a Vite `define` from `GITHUB_SHA` or `git rev-parse HEAD`, written to a `<meta>` or a `version.json` in `apps/*/vite.config.ts`), so that a deployed dist maps to a commit.
- Review note (integration tests, 13 Nov): `HealthIndependenceIT.E5_T27_healthAnswers503DownWithinItsBoundWhileTheDatabaseDoesNotAnswer` failed because it asserted a DOWN envelope of exactly 3 fields; the code change is right (the envelope is the same for UP and DOWN and now carries `commit`), so the IT now requires the `commit` field and an envelope of 4. Re-run alone: 5/5 passed.

Pass 2 (owner decision of 13 Nov 2026): real version numbers, 1.0.0 in the api (pom, health endpoint) and in the web workspaces; each web bundle ships a `version.json` with version and commit.
- `pom.xml` `<version>1.0.0</version>` (was `0.1.0-SNAPSHOT`). build-info records it and `GET /api/v1/health` answers it as `version` (UP and DOWN), with no code change in `HealthController`; the jar is `agilityhub-core-api-1.0.0.jar` (the `Dockerfile` and `bin/core` take `target/*.jar` / `agilityhub-core-api-*.jar`). The OpenAPI contract does not change (its `info.version` is the API's `v1`, and no example names the build version).
- This repository's side of `version.json`: `deploy/release-preflight.py` (`check_version`, called by `check_dist`) refuses an SPA dist without the file or with any other shape than the web's `scripts/bundle-files.mjs` writes and its `check-bundle-files.mjs` accepts: exactly the keys `version` and `commit`, `version` `x.y.z`, `commit` a full lowercase commit id or `null`. Its JSON record (`webArtifacts`) now names each bundle's `version` and `commit` next to its hash, so the release record says which web build is deployed. (Requiring a commit, i.e. a CI build, is REL-03.) Caddy needs no change: CFG-05 already serves a bundle's `version.json` as a file with `Cache-Control: no-cache`.
- Tests: new `HealthControllerTest.REL_02_healthAnswersTheReleaseVersionThePomNames` (the pom's version is `x.y.z` from 1.0.0 on, build-info records it, the health answers it; fails on `0.1.0-SNAPSHOT`, checked by reverting the pom); new `deploy/test_release_preflight.py::test_REL_02_a_dist_needs_the_version_json_that_names_its_version_and_commit` (a missing file, not JSON, not an object, a missing or an extra key, a `-SNAPSHOT` or `1.0` or numeric version, a short, upper-case or suffixed commit are refused); `test_reviewed_sha_and_registry_digest_produce_a_secret_free_artifact_record` now also asserts the version and commit in the record (both fail on the old script). Changed, not weakened: the fixture dists carry a `version.json`, so `test_CFG_04_…` counts 5 files in a dist instead of 4; `HealthCommitTest`'s fictional build-info version is `1.0.0`. Run and passing: `HealthControllerTest` (14), `HealthCommitTest` (2), `test_release_preflight.py` (33); IT `HealthIndependenceIT` (5).
- Assumption: «1.0.0 in the api» is the Maven project version; the health already returns it from build-info, so nothing else changes there. Version headings in `CHANGELOG.md`, git tags, a data-version marker and the `release` branch were not decided and are not done.
- Assumption: a dist that does not name a commit is refused by REL-03's rule, not here: this check is the shape every web build writes (`null` included), as CFG-04 and CFG-05 check theirs.
- SPEC-CHANGE: docs/DEPLOY.md step 5 (the health answers the release version as `version`) and the `bin/release-preflight` paragraph (the `version.json` refusal; the record names each bundle's version and commit). README.md «Run locally»: the health's `version` is a release version.

### REL-03 — FIXED
- `deploy/release-preflight.py` `check_dist` refuses a dist that holds `mockServiceWorker.js` anywhere: only a `VITE_MOCK=1` build copies the web's `public/` folder (`copyPublicDir: mockEnabled` in each `apps/*/vite.config.ts`, and each `public/` holds the MSW worker), so the release can no longer mount a mock build. `docs/DEPLOY.md` lists it among the refusals.

Pass 2 (owner decision of 13 Nov 2026): the production web bundles are built by CI and published as the run's artifacts; a deploy takes them only from a green run. (REL-08) The critical real-core e2e suite runs in CI on a schedule against the deployed image.
- `deploy/release-preflight.py` `check_spas`: after each dist's checks, it refuses a set of three whose `version.json` (REL-02) names no commit (`null`: a build outside CI, which writes none) or names different commits (not the three bundles of one CI run's artifact `web-bundles-<version>-<commit>`). Fixed messages, no path in them, as the other refusals.
- `docs/DEPLOY.md` step 2: the three SPAs are the artifact of a green web CI run on `main`, never a build made elsewhere: check the run (`gh run view … --json conclusion,headSha`), download its artifact (`gh run download … --name web-bundles-<version>-<commit>`), check the three `version.json` name that commit, copy `clubs/dist`, `clubs-admin/dist`, `id/dist` to versioned release directories; record the web version, commit and CI run with the image digests. Step 5 (REL-08): after the smoke, set the web repository's Actions variable `DEPLOYED_CORE_IMAGE` to the deployed image; the GHCR package must grant the web repository read access. Step 6: a rollback takes the previous release's directories (never a rebuild) and sets `DEPLOYED_CORE_IMAGE` back. The preflight paragraph and the `*_DIST_DIR` inventory row say the same.
- Tests: new `deploy/test_release_preflight.py::test_REL_03_only_the_three_bundles_of_one_ci_build_are_deployed` (one dist or all three with `commit: null` are refused; three dists that each name a commit but not the same one are refused; three of one commit pass), failing on the REL-02 script without this check. Run: `test_release_preflight.py`, 34 tests, all passing. No Java code, route or schema changed, so no IT or OpenAPI run belongs to this change.
- Assumption: «a deploy takes them only from a green run» is enforced on the host from what the bundles carry: only the web's CI release job builds with a commit (`scripts/build-release-bundles.mjs` refuses to run without `GITHUB_SHA`, and the job uploads the artifact as its last step, after `needs: checks`), and one artifact's three bundles name one commit. The preflight stays read-only and offline (it never contacts a provider), so it does not ask GitHub for the run's conclusion: the runbook's `gh run view` does. A `version.json` edited by hand would still pass; signed provenance (artifact attestations) is REL-07's open question, not decided here.
- Assumption: the same rule holds for every deployment that runs `bin/release-preflight`, the internal-review filesystem mode included; no bypass was added.
- Assumption (REL-08): the api's side of the scheduled run is the release procedure (which image the variable names, and the package access); the api's CI and its image publication are unchanged. The web side (the «Production bundles» job and `.github/workflows/real-core.yml`) is done on the web branch.
- SPEC-CHANGE: docs/DEPLOY.md steps 2, 5 and 6, the `bin/release-preflight` paragraph (the commit refusal) and the `ID_DIST_DIR`/`CLUBS_DIST_DIR`/`ADMIN_DIST_DIR` row of the environment inventory.
- OTHER-REPO: web `.github/workflows/real-core.yml` «Check the deployed api image is named» accepts only `…:sha-[0-9a-f]{40}`, but this repository's CI publishes `sha-<first 7 hex of the commit>` (`.github/workflows/ci.yml` «Publish API image»: `sha-${GITHUB_SHA::7}`), the tag `IMAGE_TAG` deploys: no image with a 40-hex tag exists, so the scheduled run can never pull the deployed image. Accept `sha-[0-9a-f]{7,40}` (the release preflight's `IMAGE` pattern), optionally followed by `@sha256:<64 hex>`.
- NOT-CODE: in the GHCR package settings of `agilityhub-core-api`, grant the web repository read access (Actions access), and set `DEPLOYED_CORE_IMAGE` at the next release. The operator's Clubs release tool (outside this repository, `docs/DEPLOY.md` «Clubs release tool») must take the three dists from the CI artifact instead of building them.
- Review note for the owner: from this commit on, `bin/release-preflight` refuses every deployment until the web's CI has produced a bundle artifact, i.e. until `release/` (the three `.env` files and the member app's install files) is committed in the web repository and its CI is green (web REL-03 review note). Bundles built today on the operator's machine name no commit and are refused, also for a hotfix.

The web's `.github/workflows/real-core.yml` accepts only `DEPLOYED_CORE_IMAGE=…:sha-<40 hex>`, while this repository's CI publishes only `sha-<7 hex>` (`.github/workflows/ci.yml` «Publish API image»), so setting the variable as docs/DEPLOY.md step 5 says makes the scheduled run fail its first check until the web accepts `sha-[0-9a-f]{7,40}` (the OTHER-REPO line above). Until then, and until the GHCR package grants the web repository read access, no scheduled real-core run against the deployed image takes place. A hand-edited `version.json` still passes the commit check (the run's conclusion is checked only by the runbook's `gh run view`).

### REL-06 — FIXED
- The handler test the auditor asks for exists since AUTHZ-03 (`ArchitectureRules.HANDLER_ROLES`: `@PreAuthorize` on every mapping or a listed open route). Pass 2 (owner decision of 13 Nov 2026): derive from MATRIU_PERMISOS the expected roles of the bearer routes that no route fixture covers and add them to the data-driven role-matrix test; list where the matrix is unclear; report a wrong permission found by the test without changing it.
- The uncovered set, computed from `docs/openapi/openapi.json` against every `e*-routes.json`: 43 bearer operations. New fixture `src/test/resources/fixtures/contracts/role-matrix-routes.json`, 53 rows (one per operation, except where the role depends on the body: `POST /attachments` per `entityType` ×3, `POST /attachments/upload-url` per `purpose` ×7, `POST /members/{id}/impersonation-token` with and without `bookingsOnly`, `POST /auth/handoff` per target app). Each row has `roles` (the matrix's reading), `scope` (CLUB, or GLOBAL for the console, the platform's account administration and the OIDC session), `impersonation` (rule 3), the request that reaches the route's own code (body, params, `Idempotency-Key` where the contract keys it, and for `/oauth2/session` the ID app's Origin, flow cookie and `id-web` audience), `matrix` (the row read) and, for a discrepancy, `current`/`currentImpersonation` plus `question`.
- New IT `configuration/RoleMatrixIT`: for every row, no bearer → 401 `UNAUTHENTICATED`; each of MEMBER, INSTRUCTOR, ADMIN, AGILITYHUB_ADMIN → passes every guard when the row admits it (its own answer, never 401, 500 or a guard's 403) and 403 with the row's refusal code (`FORBIDDEN` unless the route says otherwise) when it does not; a CLUB row refuses another club's token on the club's host (`TENANT_MISMATCH`), a GLOBAL row lets the platform's token through on a club's host; and an impersonation token (a real grant) passes only the rows whose `impersonation` is true. The role tokens are accounts with a membership of their role (the identity routes read the caller's account); the routes that work on the caller's live session (handoff, `PUT /me/password`, `PUT /me/profile`) get a real password-sign-in session; the platform administrator calls the routes the matrix gives it with its own token (no club, on a host of no club), and the others with the club in its token. Its data is removed at the start and the end (everything of its two clubs and of its accounts).
- New unit test `configuration/RoleMatrixCoverageTest`: every published bearer operation is in some `*-routes.json` (a new route without its expected roles fails), and every role-matrix row names its matrix row, uses known roles and scopes, and a recorded discrepancy still differs from the matrix and carries its question (so a decided one is not left behind). `docs/playbooks/new-endpoint.md` step 3 says so.
- Tests run: `RoleMatrixIT` (54: 53 rows + the impersonation test), `RoleMatrixCoverageTest` (2), `SecurityInventoryIT` (4), all passing. Checked that they fail when they should: with an empty fixture the coverage test fails (both cases); with `InvoiceConceptController` widened by hand to `hasAnyRole('ADMIN','MEMBER')` (reverted), `RoleMatrixIT` failed on the three `/billing/concepts` rows (MEMBER got 200, 201 and 404 instead of 403).
- Wrong permissions found (reported, NOT changed; pinned as `current` until the owner decides): (1) `POST /attachments/upload-url` `purpose: ACTIVITY_IMAGE` and (2) `ACTIVITY_DOCUMENT`: a MEMBER, and the impersonation token, gets a signed upload grant (201), though the matrix gives a member only «R publicades · RW inscripció pròpia» on activities and S07 §6 lets only ADMIN set an activity's image or documents; (3) the same route with `purpose: INSTRUCTOR_NOTE`: ADMIN gets a grant, though S03 R-03-17 makes instructors and ADMIN read-only on the note and `POST /attachments` already refuses staff for it. In all three the caller can only upload a file (allowed types, `files.maxSizeMb`) into the club's storage that no route then lets them attach: storage use, no data exposure. The fix would be `AttachmentsController.upload`'s purpose rule (`STAFF_PURPOSES` and the `anyRole("ADMIN", "MEMBER")` branch). Question for the owner: refuse those three cases (403)?
- Routes where the matrix is unclear (the reading the test takes, for the owner to confirm; where a spec rule is more specific, the test follows it):
  - `PUT /accounts/{id}/password`, `POST /platform/accounts`: no row for the platform's account administration; S01 R-01-12: AGILITYHUB_ADMIN (or the Learn client's `accounts:write` scope, which is not a role).
  - `POST /auth/handoff`: not in the matrix (its row names `/oauth2/*` and `/auth/magic-link`, W for every role); S01 R-01-13 and FE-03: into the back office an ADMIN of the club, from the back office into the club app INSTRUCTOR or ADMIN, never the club app into itself, so a MEMBER-only account has no handoff; the refusal is `NO_MEMBERSHIP`, not `FORBIDDEN`.
  - The `/me` row gives AGILITYHUB_ADMIN RW on «/me, /me/profile, canvi de perfil actiu…»: `PUT /me/profile` selects a profile of a club membership, so a platform administrator without one is refused (`NO_MEMBERSHIP`); its other `/me` routes take its own token without a club.
  - Impersonation on the account routes: rule 3 gives the token the member's permissions, S01 says `PATCH /me`, `PUT /me/password`, `/me/sessions*`, `PUT /me/onboarding`, `POST /me/onboarding/postpone` and `PUT /me/profile` are never impersonated (the account is the person's); `GET /me`, `GET /me/onboarding` and `POST /oauth2/revoke` (the banner's «Surt») accept it. The test follows S01.
  - `GET /platform/clubs/{clubId}/migrations/{runId}/report`: the console row is AGILITYHUB_ADMIN only; S18 §6 and R-18-15 also give the club's ADMIN. The test follows S18; the matrix row lacks it.
  - `DELETE /support-accesses/{id}`: the row «Accés de suport de plataforma (S17)» has ADMIN «—» and AGILITYHUB_ADMIN X; S17 R-17-10 and §6 (E123) give it to the club's ADMIN (the platform ends its own access with `POST /oauth2/revoke`). The test follows S17; the matrix row lacks E123.
  - `PUT /club`, `GET /club/holidays`, `GET /club/opening-hours` and the club pages: the row «Paràmetres, FAQ, plantilles» gives AGILITYHUB_ADMIN RW, but on the club's own routes only ADMIN has it (the console works through `/platform/clubs/{id}/…`), the reading `e2-routes.json` already applies to `GET /club` and `/parameters`; club pages (S05 §6) are not named and are read as the FAQ (MEMBER and INSTRUCTOR read, ADMIN writes).
  - `GET /calendar/time-bands`: read as «Plantilles setmanals, setmanes…» (INSTRUCTOR R, ADMIN RW). The three `POST …/export` routes: read as their `GET` twins in `e2-routes.json` (ADMIN). `GET /exports/{id}/download`: as `GET /exports/{id}` (ADMIN, MEMBER for their own package, no impersonation).
  - `POST /members/{id}/impersonation-token` with `bookingsOnly`: the matrix gives INSTRUCTOR the bookings session; an ADMIN asking for one is refused (`IMPERSONATION_DENIED`), which the matrix does not say (ADMIN has the full impersonation).
- Assumption: a route's own answer past its guards (a 404 for the fixture's missing ids, a validation or state error, `501` for the still unimplemented `POST /platform/accounts`) is what «the role passes» means; the test does not assert the business outcome, which each route's own ITs do.
- Assumption: the per-route rules in a service rather than in `@PreAuthorize` (handoff eligibility, the attachment purposes and entity types, the impersonation actor) count as the route's permission, so the fixture splits a route by the body field that changes its roles.
- SPEC-CHANGE: none to the specs or catalogues (no permission changed). docs/playbooks/new-endpoint.md step 3: a bearer route must be in a route fixture with its matrix roles.
- OTHER-REPO: none.
- Review note (review of p2-api-15-release, 14 Nov): three permission questions are open for the owner (the `current` rows of `role-matrix-routes.json`: `POST /attachments/upload-url` with `ACTIVITY_IMAGE` or `ACTIVITY_DOCUMENT` for a MEMBER and the impersonation token, and with `INSTRUCTOR_NOTE` for ADMIN); `RoleMatrixIT` pins today's behaviour, so a decision to refuse them changes `AttachmentsController.upload` and drops `current`/`currentImpersonation` from those rows in the same commit (`RoleMatrixCoverageTest` then refuses a stale `current`). The readings listed as unclear (notably ADMIN on `DELETE /support-accesses/{id}` and on the migration report, which the matrix row does not give) are tested as the code and the vertical specs have them; if the owner confirms them, MATRIU_PERMISOS needs those rows completed in the project documentation. The test proves the role guards (401, a guard's 403, another club's token); a route's own 403 with a non-guard code counts as passing, and the business outcome is left to each route's ITs. Re-run in review: `RoleMatrixIT` 54, `RoleMatrixCoverageTest` 2, `HandlerRolesSnapshotTest` 1, `SecurityInventoryIT` 4, `OpenApiSnapshotTest` 12, all passing.

## cross-repo

- `POST /oauth2/token`, `grant_type=urn:agilityhub:grant:handoff`: `PasswordGrantConverter` hands the request's `ah_refresh` cookie (`RefreshCookies.presented`: the one non-blank cookie, whatever the client or `Origin`) to `HandoffService.exchange` (new parameter `hostRefresh`, carried by `PasswordGrantAuthenticationToken` and erased with the credentials). For a code that is not an impersonation launch code (`sourceClientId` ≠ `ImpersonationService.HANDOFF_SOURCE`), `TokenService.liveSessionAccount` (new, reads only) names the account whose live session that cookie holds for the exchange's client, looked up in the host's own context before the code's club is opened (the club on a club host, none on the console's product host). In the club path and in the `console` path, after the code's own checks and just before `codes.consume`, a holder other than `code.accountId` throws `ApiException(HANDOFF_SESSION_CONFLICT)` inside the redemption's transaction: the code stays unused, no tokens are issued, and the token endpoint's failure handler answers 409 with the shared error body (`{code, message, details: {}, traceId}`, neither account named) and no `Set-Cookie` (it clears the cookie only for `REFRESH_EXPIRED`/`REFRESH_REUSED`). The same account's session, no cookie, a dead or unknown one, or another client's: as before. No new route, role, audit action, event or security event.
- Catalogue: `ErrorCode.HANDOFF_SESSION_CONFLICT(409)` with `error.HANDOFF_SESSION_CONFLICT` in `messages_ca/es/en.properties` (the web's `errors:HANDOFF_SESSION_CONFLICT` wording); `OAuthController.token` gains the 409 response's description; `docs/openapi/openapi.json` regenerated with `bin/openapi-snapshot` (only that description changes) and `docs/openapi/CHANGELOG.md` entry.
- Tests: new `HandoffSessionConflictIT` (5: B's code opened where A's club-app session is, and where A's back-office session is → 409, the shared body without details or either account, no `Set-Cookie`, no session written, the code unused, A's cookie still refreshes A, the code then redeems B without the cookie; B's own cookie and no cookie → 200 with a new cookie; a signed-out, expired, older-family-version, other-client or unknown cookie → 200; an «Entra com l'abonat» launch code (the actor A's) with B's live club-app cookie → 200, `imp` token, no `Set-Cookie`, B's cookie still refreshes B (the web's review note: this guards E47); on the console's product host a club admin's code where another platform admin's session is → 409, that session kept, then 200 without the cookie) and new unit `HandoffSessionConflictTest` (5: the refusal before consumption in both paths, same account / no or dead cookie consume as before, an impersonation code never reads the cookie, and `liveSessionAccount`'s live / revoked / older version / back office past 12 h / other client / unknown / missing account cases). With the refusal disabled, 2 IT cases and 2 unit cases fail (409 expected, 200 answered). Run and passing: `HandoffSessionConflictTest`, `HandoffServiceAppSwitchTest`, `HandoffServiceSurvivorsTest`, `ErrorCatalogContractTest`, `MessageParityTest`, and the identity package's unit tests with `ArchitectureTest`, `HandlerRolesSnapshotTest`, `RecentLoginRoutesTest` (87 classes, 327 tests, 0 failures); ITs `HandoffSessionConflictIT`, `ImpersonationHandoffIT` (14), `ConsoleProductHostIT` (11), `CookieRefreshIT` (4), `StepUpIT` (5), `SecurityHardeningIT` (5), `SecurityInventoryIT` (4), `OpenApiSnapshotTest` (12, the committed snapshot matches). No existing test changed: none sends a cookie with a handoff exchange.
- Assumption: «a live session» is the family of the cookie's refresh token, judged as `TokenService.requireFamily` judges a handoff's source session: an active token in that family (not revoked, not rotated away, not past `expiresAt`), of the account's current family version, and not past the back office's 12 hours (AUTH-01). A cookie that still holds a token rotated within a live family (a refresh answer that never arrived) counts as that live session. Account status and membership are not read: the contract names only those three conditions.
- Assumption: «the cookie of that host and client» is looked up as a refresh on that host would look it up (`RefreshTokenRepository.find` in the host's context, with the exchange's `client_id`): a cookie of the other client on the same host is not that client's session and changes nothing, as on a host without a club where no club session can be refreshed.
- Assumption: the host's cookie is the one non-blank `ah_refresh` the refresh grant would read; none or several mean no session, as the refresh grant answers them. The refresh grant's same-host `Origin`/`Referer` check is not applied: the question is what the host holds, and requiring it would let a request without `Origin` replace the session.
- Assumption: the conflict is answered after the code's own checks (alive, unused, eligible, its source session live) and just before it is consumed, so a used, expired or ineligible code answers as before (`400 HANDOFF_INVALID`, `403 NO_MEMBERSHIP`/`MEMBERSHIP_SUSPENDED`): nothing would be replaced. On the console's product host it comes before `403 PLATFORM_ONLY`; both leave the code unused.

- SPEC-CHANGE: docs/specs/S01-agilityhub-id-i-acces.md §6 `POST /oauth2/token`: `409 HANDOFF_SESSION_CONFLICT` among the answers (the web's text).
- SPEC-CHANGE: docs/specs/S01-agilityhub-id-i-acces.md T-01-12: the conflict case, plus the web's «Canvis» line of 13-11-2026.
- SPEC-CHANGE: docs/specs/00-transversal/CATALEG_ERRORS.md S01 row: new code `HANDOFF_SESSION_CONFLICT` (409) (the web's text).
- SPEC-CHANGE: docs/openapi/openapi.json `POST /oauth2/token` 409: `HANDOFF_SESSION_CONFLICT`.

### WEB-05 — FIXED
- No code changed. Lazy photos in lists need either longer-lived photo URLs than `AttachmentService.url`'s five minutes or server-side thumbnails stored next to the original with their own URL in list rows. Question: the URL lifetime (and whether unsigned thumbnails are acceptable for dog photos), the thumbnail size and format, and whether the client downscales before upload instead.

Pass 3 (closing this repository's side of the owner's decision of 13 Nov 2026): dog photos are resized in the browser before upload (the web's Pass 2, reviewed) and lists use a small thumbnail instead of the original; this repository now makes, stores, serves and erases that thumbnail, as the web entry's OTHER-REPO line asked.
- Rendition (`clubs.followup.application`, JDK `javax.imageio` only): new `ThumbnailRenderer` and `PhotoThumbnails`. A stored `image/jpeg` or `image/png` photo becomes a baseline JPEG of 256 px on its longest side (never enlarged; quality 0.82; a transparent PNG on white), written from the pixels only (the writer's own JFIF header; no EXIF, GPS, ICC profile or comment of the original) and upright: a JPEG's EXIF orientation (its single `Exif` APP1, IFD0 tag 0x0112, all eight values) is applied. Before any decoding the header is read (the JPEG markers up to EOI, the PNG chunks up to IEND) and the picture is refused over 12,000 px a side or 50 MP, or 4 MP for a JPEG its decoder holds whole (progressive, or more than one scan); a JPEG process other than SOF0/1/2, a missing EOI or IEND, a PNG `zTXt` or compressed `iTXt`, a PNG `eXIf` orientation other than 1, a malformed or repeated EXIF block, a content that is not its type, or any decoder warning (damaged data): no rendition. It is decoded with source subsampling, so the decoded raster's longest side is at most 512 px, by the JDK's own reader and writer (`com.sun.imageio.plugins`, never one a dependency registers), reading the bytes in place (no copy, no cache file).
- Bounded: one worker thread and a queue of 2 (more is answered at once with no rendition); the caller waits at most 5 s, then tells the decoder to abort (from its progress callback); the photo is read with the new `AttachmentStorage.read(key, maxBytes)` at `files.dogPhotoMaxMb` (filesystem: a bounded stream; S3: refused by its stored length before the body is read, the transfer aborted). Every failure ends in no rendition and one log line `Dog photo thumbnail not made: <REASON> (<type>)`: a fixed reason code and the type only when it is a known image type; never an id, a name, a key or a URL.
- Stored next to the original in the same store (filesystem, or S3 under `attachments/`) under its own key, with an upload grant of purpose `DOG_PHOTO_THUMBNAIL` (no route claims it) bound to the dog: the local signed route serves it like the photo (`inline`, `image/jpeg`), the S17 storage usage counts it, and the S14 erasure's sweep of the grants bound to the dog deletes it. The grant is written before the object and removed if the object cannot be stored, so a rendition left by a crash before it is bound still belongs to its dog for the erasure (and the command makes another).
- Census: `Dog.photoThumbnailFileKey` (`@ForeignOwned`: never written by a census save, no version bump, left out of the audited view in `CensusAudit`), written only by the new `DogPhotoRows`. `DocumentService.photo` (`PUT /me/dogs/{id}/photo` MEMBER, `PUT /dogs/{id}/photo` ADMIN) and `DocumentService.migrationPhoto` (B29) call the new `DogPhotoThumbnails.photoSet`: a changed photo takes the old rendition's pointer away inside the claim's transaction (one `findAndModify`, so two concurrent changes never both read it); once that commits (outside it, `PROPAGATION_NOT_SUPPORTED`, as `ErasureFilesCleanup` does), the old rendition is erased (its object, then its grant) and the new one is made and bound only while the dog, not erased, still shows that photo without one (otherwise it is erased again). Claiming the same photo again makes a missing one. The claim's answer, audit entry, event and errors are unchanged, and it never fails because of the rendition.
- Backfill: `bin/core census:photo-thumbnails --club=<slug> [--dry-run]` (new `PhotoThumbnailsCommand`, `DogPhotoThumbnails.backfill`): the club's dogs, not erased, with a photo and no rendition, by id; the dry run prints `WOULD_MAKE` for each and writes nothing; otherwise `MADE` or `NOT_MADE`; then a count. Dog ids and fixed codes only. Idempotent (a dog with a rendition is not listed, and the bind is conditional), one club, safe to re-run. `docs/DEPLOY.md` release step 5 runs it once per club.
- Erasure (R-14-15): `CensusErasureRepository.eraseDogs` unsets `photoThumbnailFileKey` and `dogPhotos` returns the rendition's key with the photo's (to `filesPending[]`); `FollowupErasure.deleteGrants` already deleted the rendition's grant and returns its key.
- API: `photoThumbnailUrl` (optional, nullable) in `DogListItem` (rows of `GET /dogs`; a `fields` key: `CensusLists`, and the `x-fields` of `GET /dogs` and `/dogs/export`), `DogSummary` (the `dogs[]` of `GET /members` rows, ADMIN and INSTRUCTOR, and of the INSTRUCTOR's `GET /members/{id}`) and `OverviewDog` (`GET /members/{id}/overview`, D10's record); `dogPhotoThumbnailUrl` beside `dogPhotoUrl` in `AttendanceRow` (`GET|PUT /class-sessions/{id}/attendance`: 21 and D12's attendance panel; `AttendanceCensusAccess.Pair` carries the key). Each is signed as the photo beside it, by the same call (`AttachmentService.url`, five minutes) in the same projection, so who may get it, the club scope and both storage modes are the photo's. Absent in the list rows without a rendition (as `photoUrl`), null in the sheet (as `dogPhotoUrl`). Detail responses (`GET /dogs/{id}`, `GET /me/dogs`, the instructor card) keep `photoUrl` only. No new route, role, audit action, event, parameter or error code. `docs/openapi/openapi.json` regenerated with `bin/openapi-snapshot` (the 4 fields and 3 `x-fields`), `docs/openapi/CHANGELOG.md` entry.
- Tests: new `ThumbnailRendererTest` (28: JPEG and PNG, landscape and portrait, a small picture never enlarged, a transparent PNG on white; the eight EXIF orientations in both byte orders, checked against the standard's table (the sides of the stored row 0 and column 0); no metadata (no APP1, APP2, APP13 or COM marker, no EXIF text or fictional camera name, a PNG `tEXt` not carried); oversized headers refused before decoding (a 50,000 × 50,000 PNG, a side over 12,000, 56 MP and 81 MP, a 4.2 MP progressive JPEG; a 0.8 MP progressive one is made); truncated JPEG and PNG, damaged entropy data; WebP, HEIC, GIF, SVG, PDF and an unknown type; a content that is not its type; malformed, repeated or out-of-range orientation and a PNG `eXIf` orientation 6; compressed PNG text; a cancelled job), `PhotoThumbnailsTest` (7: stored with a grant bound to the dog after the bounded read; an unsupported type, another dog's or another purpose's file; over the limit, missing, unreadable or undecodable; a failed store leaves no grant; the caller waits at most the limit; one at a time and a full queue answered at once; discard's order and never another file), `AttachmentStorageTest` +1 (the bounded read on both stores), `AttendanceSheetQuerySurvivorsTest` +1, and ITs `DogPhotoThumbnailsIT` (5: the claimed photo's upright rendition in every list for ADMIN and INSTRUCTOR, sparse `fields`, no rendition in detail responses or for another club, MEMBER still 403 on `GET /dogs`, unaudited, expiring; replacement erasing the old one, a re-claim changing nothing, a WebP and a truncated JPEG claimed without one; a migrated photo; the erasure; the command's dry run, apply, re-run, erased dog and bad arguments), `DogPhotoThumbnailsS3IT` (the same 5 against LocalStack's S3, plus the object under `attachments/`, its presigned URL and its deletion on replacement) and `AttendanceThumbnailIT` (1: the sheet's rows for INSTRUCTOR and ADMIN). Checked to fail without the change: with the orientation transform disabled 7 orientation cases fail, with the bounds disabled the oversized case, without the warning check the damaged case; the new fields and the command do not exist without it. Run and passing: those, `E6ResponseContractTest`, `InstructorCardQuerySurvivorsTest`, `CensusInstructorViewTest`, `ArchitectureTest`; ITs `CensusIT` (73), `ListFieldsContractIT` (4), `OpenApiSnapshotTest` (12, after the regeneration), `E6ContractIT` (31), `ErasureIT` (23), `InstructorAggregatesIT` (10), `MemberDataExportIT` (2), `UniversalListsIT` (40), `SecurityInventoryIT` (4), `RoleMatrixIT` (54), `PlayoffMigrationIT` (53, its migrated 1 × 1 PNG now also gets a rendition); the whole unit suite (538 classes, 5,362 tests, 0 failures). No existing test changed. Not run: `./mvnw verify`'s coverage gates (the whole IT suite does not fit one JVM here).
- Assumption (organizer's constraint 1, the owner may overrule it): no new dependency, only the JDK; so exactly `image/jpeg` and `image/png` photos (the type stored with the photo, as uploaded) get a rendition. WebP, HEIC/HEIF, GIF and every other type get none: the field is absent or null and the web shows the original.
- Assumption (constraint 2): the bounds above (5 s; one worker and a queue of 2; 12,000 px a side; 50 MP, or 4 MP for a progressive or multi-scan JPEG; a decoded raster of at most 512 px; `files.dogPhotoMaxMb` bytes read). Measured on this JDK: a 50 MP sequential JPEG or PNG decodes subsampled in about 0.1 s with a few MB of heap (an interlaced 50 MP PNG under a 64 MB heap too); a 50 MP progressive JPEG takes 1.3 s and about 150 MB of native memory (libjpeg keeps every DCT coefficient, up to 6 bytes a pixel), hence 4 MP (at most 24 MB) for those. At most one photo's bytes (8 MB by default) are held by the worker. The work runs after the claim commits, on the request: its answer waits at most 5 s more and is never an error.
- Assumption (constraint 3): a JPEG's EXIF orientation is applied (all eight values); a malformed or repeated `Exif` block gets no rendition. A PNG's `eXIf` orientation is not applied (browsers do not agree on it): a non-default one gets no rendition.
- Assumption (constraint 4): the rendition is a new JPEG written from pixels; it carries the JPEG writer's JFIF header only.
- Assumption (constraint 5): every test picture is drawn in the test; the EXIF block is written by hand, with a fictional camera name and fictional GPS data.
- Assumption: "erased whenever the photo is replaced" erases the replaced photo's rendition; the replaced original stays where it is today (its grant bound to the dog, deleted by the S14 erasure): the decision names the rendition only.
- Assumption: "the member record" is D10's `GET /members/{id}/overview` (the web's `CensusRecordPage` reads its `dogs[]`) and the INSTRUCTOR's `GET /members/{id}` (`MemberInstructorView.dogs`, the same `DogSummary` as the list rows); "the instructor-agenda rows" are D12's attendance panel, which reads the same `GET /class-sessions/{id}/attendance` as 21 (`GET /instructor/week` carries no photo), so one field serves both.
- Assumption: as `photoUrl`, `photoThumbnailUrl` is a `fields` key of `GET /dogs` that a sparse request names, absent in the list rows when there is none; in the sheet it is null, as `dogPhotoUrl`.
- Assumption: the command is `census:photo-thumbnails --club=<slug> [--dry-run]`, shaped as `migration:photos` (the other per-club command over the dogs' photos: it applies unless `--dry-run`). It prints dog ids and fixed outcomes only, writes no audit entry (a technical rendition), skips erased dogs and tries again, on each run, a photo that got none (`NOT_MADE`).
- Assumption: the rendition's pointer is the dog's technical, unaudited field; the audit keeps recording `photoFileKey`. The member's data package (R-14-13) dumps the dog document, so it shows `photoThumbnailFileKey` as it shows `photoFileKey`, but the rendition file is not added to `fitxers/` (the photo is).
- Assumption: on S3 the attachment principal needs `s3:DeleteObject` under `attachments/`; `docs/DEPLOY.md`'s object-storage section and its example policy, which allowed it under `signup/` only, now say so (the S14 erasure deletes photos and documents there too). Without it a replaced rendition's delete is only logged and its grant stays bound to the dog for the erasure.
- SPEC-CHANGE: docs/specs/S03-cens-abonats-gossos.md §2 row 13 and §3 `photoFileKey`: the web's text, word for word (the photo is reduced in the browser before upload; the lists show a small thumbnail made and served by the api).

No closed catalogue changes (parameters, errors, events, notifications, audit actions).

Show `photoThumbnailUrl ?? photoUrl` in the list rows: `apps/clubs-admin/src/census/CensusListPage.tsx` (dog rows: add `photoThumbnailUrl` to the `fields` it sends beside `photoUrl`; the members' `dogs[]` carry it without asking), `apps/clubs/src/instructor/StudentSearchPage.tsx` and `apps/clubs-admin/src/instructor/StudentsPage.tsx` (their `GET /dogs` rows; name it in `fields` where they send `fields`), and the member record's dogs block (`CensusRecordPage.tsx`, `overview.dogs[]`); `dogPhotoThumbnailUrl ?? dogPhotoUrl` in the rows of `apps/clubs/src/instructor/AttendancePage.tsx` and `apps/clubs-admin/src/instructor/WeekAgendaPage.tsx`, the enlarged photo keeping `dogPhotoUrl`. Exactly the photos stored as `image/jpeg` or `image/png` (the `mimeType` sent to `POST /attachments/upload-url`) get a thumbnail; `image/webp`, HEIC/HEIF, GIF and any other type get none, so the reduced photos should go up as `image/jpeg` (a canvas JPEG, baseline and without EXIF, is accepted whatever its size within the bounds above). A JPEG's EXIF orientation is honoured. The URLs last five minutes, as the photos'. No upload call changes.

### WEB-06 — NEEDS-DECISION
- No code changed. The web entry's option (b): `DELETE /parameters/{key}` taking `version` (`409 STALE_VERSION`) and an optional `reason` kept in the history, an S02 §2 contract change with the web's reset dialog. Question: options (a)–(c) of the web entry; (b) is the one that changes this repository.

### WEB-09 — NEEDS-DECISION
- No code changed. The web's late-cancel warning uses the device clock; the api decides in-time or late by its own clock (`BookingCancellationService`, `CANCELLED_LATE`), so only the dialog's text can be wrong. Options: (a) `serverNow` on the `Booking` response, either on the shared schema (every route returning a booking) or only on `GET /bookings/{id}`, as `SeatHoldResponse` has it; (b) the web reads the HTTP `Date` header of that GET, with no contract change but one-second resolution; (c) keep the device clock.

### FE-06 — NEEDS-DECISION
- No code changed. Expiring a push subscription when its session is revoked needs the session (refresh family) stored on the subscription and a hook in every revocation path (logout, `DELETE /me/sessions/{id}`, a password change, an erasure). Question: the web entry's options (a)–(c); only (b) needs this, and it would also stop push on a phone whose session simply expired.

### FE-07 — FIXED
- No code changed. The web entry's option (a) needs a build id from this repository (a `/branding` field or a response header) to compare with the SPA's own. Question: whether to tell users about a new version at all, and if so which carrier; it goes with the web's REL-02 choice of how a bundle records its commit.

Pass 2 (owner decision of 13 Nov 2026): both apps show a «new version available, reload» notice at a safe moment when the deployed build differs from the running one (compared with REL-02's `version.json`; no forced reload in the middle of a form).
- This repository's side is the delivery of `version.json`, which needs no code change: the Caddyfile's `static_files` (CFG-05) already answers an existing `*.json` as a file with `Cache-Control: no-cache` on every product host and club alias (`spa` and `club_spa`), and a missing one with 404, never the shell; REL-02's preflight refuses a release bundle without it. No build id from the API (a `/branding` field or a header, the option this entry had asked about) is needed, so no route, schema or catalogue changes.
- Tests: new `deploy/test_gateway_unavailable.py::test_FE_07_every_spa_host_serves_its_bundles_version_json_revalidated_on_every_load` (the real Caddy image: the clubs and identity product hosts and both apps behind a club alias answer their own bundle's file, `application/json`, `no-cache`; a replaced file is served at once; a bundle without it answers 404, not the shell). It pins existing behaviour, so it passes on the unchanged Caddyfile; it fails when `*.json` is dropped from the not-a-route list (checked with a modified copy through `CADDYFILE`). Run: `test_gateway_unavailable.py`, 12 tests, all passing.
- Assumption: «the deployed build» is the bundle the host serves now, read from its `version.json`; the API's own commit (REL-02's health) is not part of the comparison, since the three bundles and the image are deployed together (REL-03).
- SPEC-CHANGE: docs/PLA_FRONTEND.md §4 PWA: the «new version» rule (both apps, `version.json`, a safe moment, never a forced reload in a form). docs/DEPLOY.md «Security hardening (E11-T03)»: every app host serves its bundle's `version.json` uncached.
- OTHER-REPO: web (FE-07, package p2-web-10-rest): in `apps/clubs` and `apps/clubs-admin`, fetch `/version.json` from the app's own origin with `cache: "no-store"` (the member app's service worker must not answer it from its cache: today its precache globs leave `.json` out), at start-up, when the tab becomes visible again and on a calm interval; compare its `commit` (and `version`) with the build's own; when they differ, show «Hi ha una versió nova · Recarrega» (ca/es/en) that reloads only on the user's press, deferred while a form is open or a write is in flight; a 404, a network error or a `null` commit on either side shows nothing. No API route is involved: the file is static, served by Caddy.

### FE-10 — NEEDS-DECISION
- No code changed. `OAuthController.revoke` (`:166-170`) answers every token revoke except a bookings-only one with the Set-Cookie that clears `ah_refresh`, so leaving an impersonation or a support access signs the actor out of that host. The web entry's option (b) would clear the cookie only on the revoke of a cookie session, never for an `imp` or support token, together with a web guarantee that such a tab never restores the actor's session. Question: (a) keep today's behaviour or (b).
